### FILE: config\proxy_config\builder.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/proxy_config/builder.py

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Union

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.config.static_config.static_config import RiskStaticConfig
from riskquant.config.proxy_config.schema_objects import ProxyMap
from riskquant.config.proxy_config.proxy_config import ProxyTopology
from riskquant.config.proxy_config.loader import load_proxy_config_xlsx_into_df
from riskquant.exceptions import PrettyKeyError


def build_proxy_topology_from_df(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    static_config: RiskStaticConfig,
) -> ProxyTopology:
    """
    Parses a unified proxy mapping dataframe into a ProxyTopology,
    using RiskStaticConfig to validate names and look up integer IDs.
    """
    container_proxies: Dict[str, ProxyMap] = {}
    rf_proxies: Dict[str, ProxyMap] = {}

    # Iterate and route to the correct dictionary
    # (Note: df is assumed to be pre-filtered for active rows by the loader)
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        level = str(row["proxying_level"]).strip().lower()
        req_name = str(row["required_name"]).strip()
        proxy_name = str(row["proxy_name"]).strip()

        # Safely handle the optional scaling factor
        vol_scale = row.get("vol_scaling_factor")
        vol_scale = (
            1.0 if pd.isna(vol_scale) or vol_scale is None else float(vol_scale)
        )

        if level == "container":
            # Validate against static config
            if req_name not in static_config.container_name_to_container_id:
                raise PrettyKeyError(
                    f"Proxy Config Error: required_name '{req_name}' not found in static container metadata."
                )
            if proxy_name not in static_config.container_name_to_container_id:
                raise PrettyKeyError(
                    f"Proxy Config Error: proxy_name '{proxy_name}' not found in static container metadata."
                )

            container_proxies[req_name] = ProxyMap(
                proxying_level="container",
                required_name=req_name,
                proxy_name=proxy_name,
                vol_scaling_factor=vol_scale,
            )

        elif level == "risk_factor":
            # Validate against static config
            if req_name not in static_config.rf_name_to_rf_id:
                raise PrettyKeyError(
                    f"Proxy Config Error: required_name '{req_name}' not found in static risk factors."
                )
            if proxy_name not in static_config.rf_name_to_rf_id:
                raise PrettyKeyError(
                    f"Proxy Config Error: proxy_name '{proxy_name}' not found in static risk factors."
                )

            rf_proxies[req_name] = ProxyMap(
                proxying_level="risk_factor",
                required_name=req_name,
                proxy_name=proxy_name,
                vol_scaling_factor=vol_scale,
            )

        else:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Unknown proxying_level '{level}' for '{req_name}'. "
                "Must be strictly 'container' or 'risk_factor'."
            )

    # Construct the topology (which pre-compiles the C-level int maps)
    return ProxyTopology.build(
        container_proxies=container_proxies,
        rf_proxies=rf_proxies,
        container_name_to_container_id=static_config.container_name_to_container_id,
        rf_name_to_rf_id=static_config.rf_name_to_rf_id,
    )


# def build_proxy_topology_from_xlsx(
```

### FILE: config\proxy_config\compile.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/proxy_config/compile.py
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Union

from riskquant.config.static_config.static_config import RiskStaticConfig
from riskquant.config.proxy_config.proxy_config import ProxyTopology
from riskquant.config.proxy_config.loader import load_proxy_config_xlsx_into_df
from riskquant.config.proxy_config.validate import (
    validate_proxy_config_df,
)
from riskquant.config.proxy_config.builder import build_proxy_topology_from_df


def compile_proxy_topology_from_xlsx(
    path: Union[str, Path],
    static_config: RiskStaticConfig,
    sheet_name: Optional[str] = "proxy_config",
) -> ProxyTopology:

    # 1. Load the active proxy config rows into a DataFrame.
    proxy_df = load_proxy_config_xlsx_into_df(path=path, sheet_name=sheet_name)

    # 2. Vectorized fail-fast validation of the raw DataFrame schema and logic.
    validate_proxy_config_df(proxy_df)

    # 3. Build the topology, validating against the static config and compiling C-level int maps.
    topology = build_proxy_topology_from_df(
        df=proxy_df,
        static_config=static_config,
    )

    # Return the compiled topology object, ready for use in the system.
    return topology

```

### FILE: config\proxy_config\loader.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/proxy_config/loader.py
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Union

import pandas as pd


def load_proxy_config_xlsx_into_df(
    *,
    path: Union[str, Path],
    sheet_name: Optional[str] = "proxy_config",
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Load proxy configuration from Excel and return active rows only.
    """

    path_str = str(path)

    with pd.ExcelFile(path_str, engine="openpyxl") as xls:
        available = xls.sheet_names

        if sheet_name is not None and sheet_name not in available:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Missing required sheet '{sheet_name}' in '{path_str}'. "
                f"Available sheets: {available}"
            )

        df = pd.read_excel(xls, sheet_name=sheet_name)

    df = df.convert_dtypes()

    if "is_active" in df.columns:
        mask = df["is_active"].fillna(False).astype(bool)
        if mask.any():
            df = df[mask]
            df = df.drop(columns=["is_active"])
            df = df.reset_index(drop=True)

    return df

```

### FILE: config\proxy_config\proxy_config.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/proxy_config/proxy_config.py

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, Optional, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.config.proxy_config.schema_objects import ProxyMap


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class ProxyTopology:
    """
    Immutable container for proxy mappings.
    Maintains both the human-readable string maps and the compiled integer maps
    required for fast C-level kernel execution.
    """

    container_proxies_dict: Dict[str, ProxyMap]
    risk_factor_proxies_dict: Dict[str, ProxyMap]

    # Stored lightweight mappings for easy dataframe visualization and override resolution
    container_name_to_container_id: Dict[str, int]
    rf_name_to_rf_id: Dict[str, int]

    # Pre-compiled for O(1) integer kernel access: required_id -> (proxy_id, scaling_factor)
    container_id_to_proxy_container_id: Dict[int, Tuple[int, float]]
    rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id: Dict[int, Tuple[int, float]]

    @classmethod
    def build(
        cls,
        container_proxies: Dict[str, ProxyMap],
        rf_proxies: Dict[str, ProxyMap],
        container_name_to_container_id: Dict[str, int],
        rf_name_to_rf_id: Dict[str, int],
    ) -> "ProxyTopology":
        """Factory method to construct ProxyTopology with pre-compiled integer maps."""

        # Build container ID to proxy container ID map (including multiplier)
        container_id_to_proxy_container_id = {
            container_name_to_container_id[cp.required_name]: (
                container_name_to_container_id[cp.proxy_name],
                float(
                    cp.vol_scaling_factor
                    if cp.vol_scaling_factor is not None
                    else 1.0
                ),
            )
            for cp in container_proxies.values()
        }

        # Build risk factor ID to proxy risk factor ID map (including multiplier)
        rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id = {
            rf_name_to_rf_id[rp.required_name]: (
                rf_name_to_rf_id[rp.proxy_name],
                float(
                    rp.vol_scaling_factor
                    if rp.vol_scaling_factor is not None
                    else 1.0
                ),
            )
            for rp in rf_proxies.values()
        }

        return cls(
            container_proxies_dict=container_proxies,
            risk_factor_proxies_dict=rf_proxies,
            container_name_to_container_id=container_name_to_container_id,
            rf_name_to_rf_id=rf_name_to_rf_id,
            container_id_to_proxy_container_id=container_id_to_proxy_container_id,
            rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id=rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id,
        )

    def with_overrides(
        self,
        container_proxy_overrides: Optional[Dict[str, ProxyMap]] = None,
        rf_proxy_overrides: Optional[Dict[str, ProxyMap]] = None,
        # Optional updated mappings, defaults to existing ones if not provided
        container_name_to_container_id: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None,
        rf_name_to_rf_id: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None,
    ) -> "ProxyTopology":
        """
        Creates a new ProxyTopology by safely layering overrides on top of the base defaults.
        Recompiles the fast integer maps automatically.
        """
        new_containers = dict(self.container_proxies_dict)
        if container_proxy_overrides:
            new_containers.update(container_proxy_overrides)

        new_rfs = dict(self.risk_factor_proxies_dict)
        if rf_proxy_overrides:
            new_rfs.update(rf_proxy_overrides)

        return ProxyTopology.build(
            container_proxies=new_containers,
            rf_proxies=new_rfs,
            container_name_to_container_id=container_name_to_container_id
            or self.container_name_to_container_id,
            rf_name_to_rf_id=rf_name_to_rf_id or self.rf_name_to_rf_id,
        )

    def to_df(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """
        Consolidates both container and risk factor proxies into a single
        pandas DataFrame for easy visualization and auditing.
        """
        records = []

        # Process container proxies
        for cp in self.container_proxies_dict.values():
            records.append(
                {
                    "proxying_level": cp.proxying_level,
                    "required_name": cp.required_name,
                    "required_name_id": self.container_name_to_container_id[
                        cp.required_name
                    ],
                    "proxy_name": cp.proxy_name,
                    "proxy_name_id": self.container_name_to_container_id[
                        cp.proxy_name
                    ],
                    "vol_scaling_factor": float(
                        cp.vol_scaling_factor
                        if cp.vol_scaling_factor is not None
                        else 1.0
                    ),
                }
            )

        # Process risk factor proxies
        for rp in self.risk_factor_proxies_dict.values():
            records.append(
                {
                    "proxying_level": rp.proxying_level,
                    "required_name": rp.required_name,
                    "required_name_id": self.rf_name_to_rf_id[rp.required_name],
                    "proxy_name": rp.proxy_name,
                    "proxy_name_id": self.rf_name_to_rf_id[rp.proxy_name],
                    "vol_scaling_factor": float(
                        rp.vol_scaling_factor
                        if rp.vol_scaling_factor is not None
                        else 1.0
                    ),
                }
            )

        df = pd.DataFrame(
            records,
            columns=[
                "proxying_level",
                "required_name",
                "required_name_id",
                "proxy_name",
                "proxy_name_id",
                "vol_scaling_factor",
            ],
        )
        return df

    @property
    def df(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Alias for .to_df() for quick notebook access."""
        return self.to_df()

```

### FILE: config\proxy_config\validate.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/proxy_config/validate.py

import pandas as pd


def validate_proxy_config_df(df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    """
    Performs vectorized validation on the raw proxy configuration DataFrame.
    Checks for schema correctness, uniqueness constraints, and logical errors.

    Args:
        df: The active rows of the proxy configuration DataFrame.

    Raises:
        ValueError: If any validation constraints are violated.
    """
    if df.empty:
        return  # An empty proxy map is valid (no proxies applied)

    # 1. Check Required Columns
    required_columns = {"proxying_level", "required_name", "proxy_name"}
    missing_cols = required_columns - set(df.columns)
    if missing_cols:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Proxy config validation failed: Missing required columns {missing_cols}. "
            f"Found columns: {list(df.columns)}"
        )

    # 2. Check for Missing/Null Values in Key Columns
    has_nulls = (
        df[["proxying_level", "required_name", "proxy_name"]].isnull().any()
    )
    if has_nulls.any():
        null_cols = has_nulls[has_nulls].index.tolist()
        raise ValueError(
            f"Proxy config validation failed: Found null values in required columns: {null_cols}. "
            "Names and proxying levels cannot be blank."
        )

    # 3. Check Domain Constraints on proxying_level
    valid_levels = {"container", "risk_factor"}
    invalid_levels = df[~df["proxying_level"].isin(valid_levels)]
    if not invalid_levels.empty:
        bad_values = invalid_levels["proxying_level"].unique().tolist()
        raise ValueError(
            f"Proxy config validation failed: Invalid proxying_level(s) found: {bad_values}. "
            f"Must be one of {valid_levels}."
        )

    # 4. Check Uniqueness Constraints
    # A single required_name cannot be proxied to multiple different proxies within the same level
    duplicates = df[
        df.duplicated(subset=["proxying_level", "required_name"], keep=False)
    ]
    if not duplicates.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Proxy config validation failed: Found duplicate mapping rules for the same required_name. "
            f"Conflicting rows:\n{duplicates[['proxying_level', 'required_name', 'proxy_name']].to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # 5. Check Logical Constraints (Self-Referencing)
    # A proxy cannot proxy to itself (this would create a pointless loop)
    self_referencing = df[df["required_name"] == df["proxy_name"]]
    if not self_referencing.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Proxy config validation failed: A required_name cannot proxy to itself. "
            f"Invalid rows:\n{self_referencing[['proxying_level', 'required_name']].to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # 6. Required names should not be duplicated across levels (e.g. a container and a risk factor with the same name)
    name_counts = df["required_name"].value_counts()
    duplicated_names = name_counts[name_counts > 1].index.tolist()
    if duplicated_names:
        raise ValueError(
            "Proxy config validation failed: Found required_name(s) duplicated across proxying levels. "
            f"Duplicated names: {duplicated_names}. Required names must be unique across the entire config."
        )

```
