### FILE: config\static_config\builder.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/builder.py
"""
Builder module for risk configuration.
Isolates Pandas and DataFrame memory from the runtime config objects.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.config.static_config.static_config import RiskStaticConfig
from riskquant.config.static_config.schema_objects import (
    AliasSchema,
    AssetClass,
    AssetSubclass,
    BusinessDayConvention,
    Ccy,
    CompoundingConvention,
    ContainerMeta,
    DayCountConvention,
    ExtrapolationMethod,
    HolidayCalendar,
    InterpolationMethod,
    RiskFactor,
    ShiftType,
    SpotRollRule,
    SurfaceYAxisType,
    TenorSchema,
    UnitScale,
    UnitType,
)
from riskquant.market_data.risk_factors.shift_types import (
    validate_shift_types_dict,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RiskStaticConfigFrames:
    """
    Temporary container that holds pandas DataFrames loaded from Excel.
    Call .compile() to create a runtime RiskStaticConfig that holds no DataFrames.

    The tab names in the Excel workbook must match the field names in this dataclass, which are:
    - container_meta,
    - risk_factors,
    - tenor_schema,
    - alias_schema,
    - spot_roll_rules,
    - asset_classes,
    - asset_subclasses,
    - unit_types,
    - unit_scales,
    - surface_y_axis_schema,
    - ccy,
    - shift_types,
    - interpolation_methods,
    - extrapolation_methods,
    - holiday_calendars,
    - day_count_conventions,
    - business_day_conventions,
    - compounding_conventions.

    """

    # Core
    container_meta: pd.DataFrame
    risk_factors: pd.DataFrame

    # Schemas
    tenor_schema: pd.DataFrame
    alias_schema: pd.DataFrame
    spot_roll_rules: pd.DataFrame
    surface_y_axis_schema: pd.DataFrame

    # Lookups
    asset_classes: pd.DataFrame
    asset_subclasses: pd.DataFrame
    unit_types: pd.DataFrame
    unit_scales: pd.DataFrame
    ccy: pd.DataFrame
    shift_types: pd.DataFrame
    interpolation_methods: pd.DataFrame
    extrapolation_methods: pd.DataFrame

    # Calendars / conventions
    holiday_calendars: pd.DataFrame
    day_count_conventions: pd.DataFrame
    business_day_conventions: pd.DataFrame
    compounding_conventions: pd.DataFrame

    def compile(self) -> RiskStaticConfig:
        """
        Compile validated DataFrames into compact Python structures and return runtime config.
        """

        # --- Helper for fast, null-safe DataFrame iteration ---
        def _iter_df(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list[dict]:
            if df is None or df.empty:
                return []
            # Replaces pandas <NA> and np.nan with native Python None for clean extraction
            return df.where(pd.notnull(df), None).to_dict("records")

        # ======================= #
        # --- LOOKUPS & CONVS --- #
        # ======================= #
        asset_classes_dict = {
            str(r["asset_class"]): AssetClass(
                asset_class=str(r["asset_class"]),
                asset_class_id=int(r["asset_class_id"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.asset_classes)
        }

        asset_subclasses_dict = {
            str(r["asset_subclass"]): AssetSubclass(
                asset_subclass=str(r["asset_subclass"]),
                asset_subclass_id=int(r["asset_subclass_id"]),
                asset_class=str(r["asset_class"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.asset_subclasses)
        }

        unit_types_dict = {
            str(r["unit_type_name"]): UnitType(
                unit_type_name=str(r["unit_type_name"]),
                unit_type_name_id=int(r["unit_type_name_id"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.unit_types)
        }

        unit_scales_dict = {
            str(r["unit_scale_name"]): UnitScale(
                unit_scale_name=str(r["unit_scale_name"]),
                unit_scale_name_id=int(r["unit_scale_name_id"]),
                scale_to_base=float(r["scale_to_base"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.unit_scales)
        }

        day_count_conventions_dict = {
            str(r["day_count_convention"]): DayCountConvention(
                day_count_convention=str(r["day_count_convention"]),
                day_count_convention_id=int(r["day_count_convention_id"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.day_count_conventions)
        }

        business_day_conventions_dict = {
            str(r["business_day_convention"]): BusinessDayConvention(
                business_day_convention=str(r["business_day_convention"]),
                business_day_convention_id=int(r["business_day_convention_id"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.business_day_conventions)
        }

        compounding_conventions_dict = {
            str(r["compounding_convention"]): CompoundingConvention(
                compounding_convention=str(r["compounding_convention"]),
                compounding_convention_id=int(r["compounding_convention_id"]),
                compounding_type=str(r["compounding_type"]),
                frequency_per_year=(
                    float(r["frequency_per_year"])
                    if r.get("frequency_per_year") is not None
                    else None
                ),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.compounding_conventions)
        }

        interpolation_methods_dict = {
            str(r["interpolation_method"]): InterpolationMethod(
                interpolation_method=str(r["interpolation_method"]),
                interpolation_method_id=int(r["interpolation_method_id"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.interpolation_methods)
        }

        extrapolation_methods_dict = {
            str(r["extrapolation_method"]): ExtrapolationMethod(
                extrapolation_method=str(r["extrapolation_method"]),
                extrapolation_method_id=int(r["extrapolation_method_id"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.extrapolation_methods)
        }

        # Fixed: Now mapping using the actual schema objects
        ccy_dict = {
            str(r["ccy"]).upper(): Ccy(
                ccy=str(r["ccy"]).upper(), ccy_id=int(r["ccy_id"])
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.ccy)
        }

        shift_types_dict = {
            str(r["shift_type"]).upper(): ShiftType(
                shift_type=str(r["shift_type"]).upper(),
                shift_type_id=int(r["shift_type_id"]),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.shift_types)
        }
        # Pass a mapped `{str: int}` dict safely into the validator to prevent it from breaking
        validate_shift_types_dict(
            {k: v.shift_type_id for k, v in shift_types_dict.items()}
        )

        # HOLIDAY CALENDARS

        # Get df.
        holiday_df = self.holiday_calendars

        # print(holiday_df.to_string())

        # Assuming df is your loaded holiday calendar DataFrame
        # 1. Ensure the dates are actual Python date objects (not pandas Timestamps or strings)
        holiday_df["holiday_date"] = pd.to_datetime(
            holiday_df["holiday_date"]
        ).dt.date

        # 2. Group by the calendar name and aggregate the dates into a set
        # We also rename the output column to 'holiday_dates' to match builder.py
        aggregated_calendars = (
            holiday_df.groupby("holiday_calendar_name")["holiday_date"]
            .apply(set)
            .reset_index(name="holiday_dates")
        )

        # 3. Now you can convert this to a list of dicts to feed your builder!
        calendar_records = aggregated_calendars.to_dict(orient="records")

        # Example of what calendar_records looks like now:
        # [
        #     {'holiday_calendar_name': 'NL', 'holiday_dates': {datetime.date(2026, 1, 1)}},
        #     {'holiday_calendar_name': 'US', 'holiday_dates': {datetime.date(2026, 1, 1)}}
        # ]

        # Instantiating the HolidayCalendar schema
        holiday_calendars_dict = {
            str(r["holiday_calendar_name"]): HolidayCalendar(
                holiday_calendar_name=str(r["holiday_calendar_name"]),
                holiday_dates=(
                    set(r["holiday_dates"])
                    if r.get("holiday_dates") is not None
                    else set()
                ),
            )
            for r in calendar_records
        }

        # ======================= #
        # --- CORE META & RF  --- #
        # ======================= #
        container_meta_dict: Dict[str, ContainerMeta] = {}
        for r in _iter_df(self.container_meta):
            if not bool(r.get("active", 1)):
                continue

            c_name = str(r["container_name"])
            base_ccy = r.get("base_ccy")
            quote_ccy = str(r["quote_ccy"])
            base_ccy = str(base_ccy) if base_ccy is not None else quote_ccy

            container_meta_dict[c_name] = ContainerMeta(
                container_name=c_name,
                container_name_id=int(r["container_name_id"]),
                base_ccy=base_ccy,
                quote_ccy=quote_ccy,
                unit_type_name=str(r["unit_type_name"]),
                unit_scale_name=str(r["unit_scale_name"]),
                container_dim=int(r["container_dim"]),
                asset_class=str(r["asset_class"]),
                asset_subclass=str(r["asset_subclass"]),
                tenor_type_name=r.get("tenor_type_name"),
                spot_roll_rule_name=r.get("spot_roll_rule_name"),
                alias_type_name=r.get("alias_type_name"),
                surface_y_axis_type_name=r.get("surface_y_axis_type_name"),
                shift_type_name=str(r["shift_type_name"]),
                interp_method_name_x=r.get("interp_method_name_x"),
                interp_method_name_y=r.get("interp_method_name_y"),
                extrap_method_name_x=r.get("extrap_method_name_x"),
                extrap_method_name_y=r.get("extrap_method_name_y"),
            )

        risk_factors_dict: Dict[str, RiskFactor] = {}
        for r in _iter_df(self.risk_factors):
            if not bool(r.get("active", 1)):
                continue

            rf_name = str(r["risk_factor_name"])
            risk_factors_dict[rf_name] = RiskFactor(
                risk_factor_name=rf_name,
                risk_factor_name_id=int(r["risk_factor_name_id"]),
                tenor_token=r.get("tenor_token"),
                container_name=str(r["container_name"]),
            )

        # ======================= #
        # --- SCHEMAS / RULES --- #
        # ======================= #

        # TENOR SCHEMA
        tenor_schema_dict = {
            str(r["tenor_type_name"]): TenorSchema(
                tenor_type_name=str(r["tenor_type_name"]),
                tenor_unit=str(r["tenor_unit"]),
                digits=int(r["digits"]),
                token_regex=(
                    re.compile(str(r["token_regex"]))
                    if pd.notna(r.get("token_regex"))
                    else None
                ),
                abs_name_regex=(
                    re.compile(str(r["abs_name_regex"]))
                    if pd.notna(r.get("abs_name_regex"))
                    else None
                ),
                rel_name_regex=(
                    re.compile(str(r["rel_name_regex"]))
                    if pd.notna(r.get("rel_name_regex"))
                    else None
                ),
                abs_name_example=str(r.get("abs_name_example", "")),
                rel_name_example=str(r.get("rel_name_example", "")),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.tenor_schema)
        }

        # ALIAS SCHEMA
        alias_schema_dict = {
            str(r["alias_type_name"]): AliasSchema(
                alias_type_name=str(r["alias_type_name"]),
                alias_kind=str(r["alias_kind"]),
                alias_unit=r.get("alias_unit"),
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.alias_schema)
        }

        # SURFACE Y-AXIS SCHEMA
        surface_y_axis_schema_dict = {
            str(r["y_axis_type_name"]): SurfaceYAxisType(
                y_axis_type_name=str(r["y_axis_type_name"])
            )
            for r in _iter_df(self.surface_y_axis_schema)
        }

        # SPOT ROLL RULES SCHEMA
        spot_roll_rules_dict: Dict[Tuple[str, str], SpotRollRule] = {}
        for r in _iter_df(self.spot_roll_rules):
            key = (str(r["spot_roll_rule_name"]), str(r["tenor_type_name"]))
            spot_roll_rules_dict[key] = SpotRollRule(
                spot_roll_rule_name=key[0],
                tenor_type_name=key[1],
                spot_roll_method=str(r["spot_roll_method"]),
                offset_in_tenor_units=r.get("offset_in_tenor_units"),
                roll_month=r.get("roll_month"),
                roll_day=r.get("roll_day"),
                fh_bh_ddmm=r.get("fh_bh_ddmm"),
                post_roll_shift=r.get("post_roll_shift"),
            )

        # # ======================= #
        # # --- PROXIES         --- #
        # # ======================= #

        # # Container proxies
        # container_proxies_dict = {
        #     str(r["container_name"]): ContainerProxyMap(
        #         container_name=str(r["container_name"]),
        #         proxy_container_name=str(r["proxy_container_name"]),
        #         proxy_container_vol_scaling_factor=float(
        #             r.get("proxy_container_vol_scaling_factor", 1.0) or 1.0
        #         ),
        #     )
        #     for r in _iter_df(self.container_proxies)
        # }

        # # Risk factor proxies
        # risk_factor_proxies_dict = {
        #     str(r["risk_factor_name"]): RiskFactorProxyMap(
        #         risk_factor_name=str(r["risk_factor_name"]),
        #         proxy_risk_factor_name=str(r["proxy_risk_factor_name"]),
        #         proxy_risk_factor_vol_scaling_factor=float(
        #             r.get("proxy_risk_factor_vol_scaling_factor", 1.0) or 1.0
        #         ),
        #     )
        #     for r in _iter_df(self.risk_factor_proxies)
        # }

        # ======================= #
        # --- FINALIZE CONFIG --- #
        # ======================= #
        return RiskStaticConfig(
            container_meta_dict=container_meta_dict,
            risk_factors_dict=risk_factors_dict,
            tenor_schema_dict=tenor_schema_dict,
            alias_schema_dict=alias_schema_dict,
            spot_roll_rules_dict=spot_roll_rules_dict,
            surface_y_axis_schema_dict=surface_y_axis_schema_dict,
            asset_classes_dict=asset_classes_dict,
            asset_subclasses_dict=asset_subclasses_dict,
            unit_types_dict=unit_types_dict,
            unit_scales_dict=unit_scales_dict,
            day_count_conventions_dict=day_count_conventions_dict,
            business_day_conventions_dict=business_day_conventions_dict,
            compounding_conventions_dict=compounding_conventions_dict,
            interpolation_methods_dict=interpolation_methods_dict,
            extrapolation_methods_dict=extrapolation_methods_dict,
            ccy_dict=ccy_dict,
            shift_types_dict=shift_types_dict,
            holiday_calendars_dict=holiday_calendars_dict,
        )

```

### FILE: config\static_config\compile.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/compile.py
"""
Main API for compiling configurations from source data (e.g. .xlsx workbooks)
into runtime config objects.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Union


from riskquant.config.static_config.builder import RiskStaticConfigFrames
from riskquant.config.static_config.static_config import RiskStaticConfig
from riskquant.config.static_config.loader import (
    load_risk_static_data_config_xlsx_into_dict,
)
from riskquant.config.static_config.validate import validate_static_config_dfs


def compile_risk_static_config_from_xlsx(
    path: Union[str, Path],
) -> RiskStaticConfig:
    """
    Load, validate, and compile a risk static config .xlsx workbook into a
    RiskStaticConfig instance.

    Args:
        path: Path to the Excel (.xlsx) workbook containing the static config sheets.

    Returns:
        RiskStaticConfig: the compiled runtime configuration object.

    Raises:
        ValueError: if required sheets are missing, or validation fails on
            uniqueness/referential integrity constraints.
    """

    # 1. Load config dataframes into a dictionary (with optimized dtypes).
    dict_of_dfs = load_risk_static_data_config_xlsx_into_dict(path=path)

    # 2. Vectorized fail-fast validation of raw DataFrames (C-level uniqueness & FK checks).
    validate_static_config_dfs(dict_of_dfs)

    # 3. Create the lightweight frames container (now stripped of heavy validation logic).
    frames_config = RiskStaticConfigFrames(**dict_of_dfs)

    # 4. Compile the DataFrames into our highly efficient, slot-based python structures.
    compiled_config = frames_config.compile()

    # 5. Return the compiled config object ready for use in runtime/model code.
    return compiled_config

```

### FILE: config\static_config\loader.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/loader.py
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Final, Iterable, Mapping, Optional, Union

import pandas as pd


def write_config_dict_to_llm_txt(
    *,
    data: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
    output_path: Union[str, Path],
) -> None:
    """
    Serialize config DataFrames into an LLM-friendly text format.

    Design goals:
        - Deterministic
        - Token efficient
        - Easily parsable by LLMs
        - No dataframe truncation
        - Explicit structure boundaries
    """
    output_path = Path(output_path)

    with output_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:

        for sheet_name, df in data.items():

            # Ensure stable column order
            df = df.copy(deep=False)

            f.write(f"=== SHEET: {sheet_name} ===\n")
            f.write(f"ROWS: {len(df)}\n")
            f.write("COLUMNS: " + ", ".join(map(str, df.columns)) + "\n\n")

            # CSV is extremely LLM-friendly.
            # Use na_rep="" instead of .fillna("") to safely handle strict dtypes like Int64.
            df.to_csv(
                f,
                index=False,
                lineterminator="\n",
                na_rep="",
            )

            f.write("\n=== END SHEET ===\n\n")


def load_risk_static_data_config_xlsx_into_dict(
    *,
    path: Union[str, Path],
    sheet_names: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,
) -> Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Load selected sheets from a config workbook.

    Notebook-safe:
      - Ensures the Excel file handle is closed so the workbook can be saved/edited after load.
    Memory/CPU efficient:
      - Reads selected sheets in one call where possible.
      - Avoids unnecessary copies.
    """
    path_str = str(path)

    # Use a context manager to guarantee the file handle is closed
    with pd.ExcelFile(path_str, engine="openpyxl") as xls:
        available = xls.sheet_names

        if sheet_names is None:
            sheets = available  # reuse list
        else:
            # materialize once; also ensures list-like -> dict return
            sheets = tuple(sheet_names)

        missing = tuple(s for s in sheets if s not in available)
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Missing required sheet(s) in '{path_str}': {missing}"
            )

        # One read call for all requested sheets
        dict_of_dfs = pd.read_excel(xls, sheet_name=sheets)

    # Validate that dfs is a mapping of sheet_name -> DataFrame
    if not isinstance(dict_of_dfs, Mapping):
        raise ValueError(
            "Expected multiple sheets to return a Mapping[str, DataFrame]."
        )

    # Clean the DataFrames to ensure consistent dtypes and memory layouts.
    clean_dict_of_dfs = {}
    for sheet_name, df in dict_of_dfs.items():
        # Convert dtypes: floats with NaNs become nullable Int64, objects become strings.
        # This prevents precision leaks and enforces tight memory layouts.
        clean_dict_of_dfs[sheet_name] = df.convert_dtypes()

    # Return a regular dict to avoid potential issues with pandas' internal dict subclass
    return clean_dict_of_dfs

```

### FILE: config\static_config\resolution.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/resolution.py
from __future__ import annotations

from bisect import bisect_left
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date


from collections.abc import Iterable, Iterator, Mapping
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import overload

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# Ensure these utility functions are available in riskquant.utils.tenors
from riskquant.config.static_config.static_config import RiskStaticConfig
from riskquant.utils.tenors import (
    add_months_to_date_and_return_yyyymm01,
    fmt_date_as_str_yyyymm,
    get_spot_year,
    parse_bracket_token,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class RiskFactorAbsToRelNameIdMap(Mapping[str, tuple[str, int]]):
    as_of_date: date
    abs_to_rel_name_and_id: Mapping[str, tuple[str, int]]  # abs -> (rel, id)

    # NO INIT

    # Inverted mapping for convenient reverse lookups: rel -> (abs, id)
    _rel_to_abs_name_and_id: Mapping[str, tuple[str, int]] = field(
        init=False,
        repr=False,
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:

        normalized = dict(self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id)

        seen_ids: dict[int, str] = {}
        seen_rel_names: dict[str, str] = {}

        for abs_name, (rel_name, rf_id) in normalized.items():
            if not isinstance(abs_name, str):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"absolute name must be str, got {type(abs_name).__name__}"
                )

            if not isinstance(rel_name, str):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"rel_name for '{abs_name}' must be str, got {type(rel_name).__name__}"
                )

            if not isinstance(rf_id, (int, np.integer)):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"id for '{abs_name}' must be an integer, got {type(rf_id).__name__}={rf_id!r}"
                )

            iv = int(rf_id)

            prev_abs_for_id = seen_ids.get(iv)
            if prev_abs_for_id is not None and prev_abs_for_id != abs_name:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Mapping contains duplicate ID assignment:\n"
                    f"  '{prev_abs_for_id}' and '{abs_name}' both map to ID={iv}\n"
                    "Each absolute risk factor must map to a unique risk_factor_name_id."
                )
            seen_ids[iv] = abs_name

            prev_abs_for_rel = seen_rel_names.get(rel_name)
            if prev_abs_for_rel is not None and prev_abs_for_rel != abs_name:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Mapping contains duplicate relative risk factor name assignment:\n"
                    f"  '{prev_abs_for_rel}' and '{abs_name}' both map to rel_name='{rel_name}'\n"
                    "Each absolute risk factor must map to a unique relative risk factor name."
                )
            seen_rel_names[rel_name] = abs_name

        # Construct the reverse mapping for rel_name -> (abs_name, id)
        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "abs_to_rel_name_and_id",
            MappingProxyType(normalized),
        )

        # Construct the reverse mapping for rel_name -> (abs_name, id)
        reverse = {
            rel_name: (abs_name, int(rf_id))
            for abs_name, (rel_name, rf_id) in normalized.items()
        }
        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "_rel_to_abs_name_and_id",
            MappingProxyType(reverse),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def from_config(
        config: RiskStaticConfig,
        as_of_date: date,
    ) -> "RiskFactorAbsToRelNameIdMap":
        return build_risk_factor_name_mapping_for_as_of_date(
            config=config,
            as_of_date=as_of_date,
        )

    def __getitem__(self, abs_name: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
        return self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id[abs_name]

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[str]:
        return iter(self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id)

    @overload
    def lookup(self, abs_name: str) -> tuple[str, int]: ...
    @overload
    def lookup(self, abs_name: Iterable[str]) -> list[tuple[str, int]]: ...

    def lookup(
        self,
        abs_name: str | Iterable[str],
    ) -> tuple[str, int] | list[tuple[str, int]]:
        m = self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id
        if isinstance(abs_name, str):
            return m[abs_name]
        return [m[a] for a in abs_name]

    @overload
    def get_rel_name_for_abs_name(self, abs_name: str) -> str: ...
    @overload
    def get_rel_name_for_abs_name(
        self, abs_name: Iterable[str]
    ) -> list[str]: ...

    def get_rel_name_for_abs_name(
        self,
        abs_name: str | Iterable[str],
    ) -> str | list[str]:
        m = self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id
        if isinstance(abs_name, str):
            return m[abs_name][0]
        return [m[a][0] for a in abs_name]

    @overload
    def get_rel_id_for_abs_name(self, abs_name: str) -> int: ...
    @overload
    def get_rel_id_for_abs_name(self, abs_name: Iterable[str]) -> list[int]: ...

    def get_rel_id_for_abs_name(
        self,
        abs_name: str | Iterable[str],
    ) -> int | list[int]:
        m = self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id
        if isinstance(abs_name, str):
            return int(m[abs_name][1])
        return [int(m[a][1]) for a in abs_name]

    def to_df(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        items = sorted(self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id.items(), key=lambda x: x[0])
        abs_names = [k for k, _ in items]
        rel_names = [v[0] for _, v in items]
        ids = np.fromiter(
            (int(v[1]) for _, v in items),
            dtype=np.int32,
            count=len(items),
        )

        return pd.DataFrame(
            {
                "risk_factor_name": rel_names,
                "risk_factor_id": ids,
            },
            index=pd.Index(abs_names, name="absolute_risk_factor_name"),
        )

    @property
    def df(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Alias for to_df(), for convenient access."""
        return self.to_df()

    @property
    def abs_to_rel_name(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {
            abs_name: rf_name
            for abs_name, (rf_name, _) in self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id.items()
        }

    @property
    def abs_to_rel_id(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return {
            abs_name: int(rf_id)
            for abs_name, (_, rf_id) in self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id.items()
        }

    @property
    def rel_to_abs_name(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {
            rf_name: abs_name
            for abs_name, (rf_name, _) in self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id.items()
        }

    @property
    def rel_name_to_id(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return {
            rf_name: int(rf_id)
            for _, (rf_name, rf_id) in self.abs_to_rel_name_and_id.items()
        }

    # --- INVERTED MAPPINGS FOR CONVENIENCE --- #

    # rel -> (abs, id)

    @property
    def rel_to_abs_name_and_id(self) -> Mapping[str, tuple[str, int]]:
        return self._rel_to_abs_name_and_id

    # Scalar lookups: rel_name -> (abs_name, id)
    @overload
    def get_abs_name_and_id_for_rel_name(
        self, rel_name: str
    ) -> tuple[str, int]: ...
    @overload
    def get_abs_name_and_id_for_rel_name(
        self, rel_name: Iterable[str]
    ) -> list[tuple[str, int]]: ...

    def get_abs_name_and_id_for_rel_name(
        self,
        rel_name: str | Iterable[str],
    ) -> tuple[str, int] | list[tuple[str, int]]:
        m = self.rel_to_abs_name_and_id
        if isinstance(rel_name, str):
            return m[rel_name]
        return [m[r] for r in rel_name]

    # Scalar lookups: rel_name -> abs_name
    @overload
    def get_abs_name_for_rel_name(
        self,
        rel_name: str,
    ) -> str: ...
    @overload
    def get_abs_name_for_rel_name(
        self,
        rel_name: Iterable[str],
    ) -> list[str]: ...

    def get_abs_name_for_rel_name(
        self,
        rel_name: str | Iterable[str],
    ) -> str | list[str]:
        m = self.rel_to_abs_name_and_id
        if isinstance(rel_name, str):
            return m[rel_name][0]
        return [m[r][0] for r in rel_name]

    # Scalar lookups: rel_name -> id
    @overload
    def get_id_for_rel_name(
        self,
        rel_name: str,
    ) -> int: ...
    @overload
    def get_id_for_rel_name(
        self,
        rel_name: Iterable[str],
    ) -> list[int]: ...

    def get_id_for_rel_name(
        self,
        rel_name: str | Iterable[str],
    ) -> int | list[int]:
        m = self.rel_to_abs_name_and_id
        if isinstance(rel_name, str):
            return int(m[rel_name][1])
        return [int(m[r][1]) for r in rel_name]


def build_risk_factor_name_mapping_for_as_of_date(
    *,
    config: RiskStaticConfig,
    as_of_date: date,
) -> RiskFactorAbsToRelNameIdMap:
    """
    Resolve relative risk factor names into absolute names for a given as_of_date.

    Design:
        - Interrogates `config.risk_factors_dict` to define the universe of valid factors.
        - Translates each relative factor to its absolute name based on spot roll rules.
    """
    abs_to_rel_name_and_id: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}

    for rf in config.risk_factors_dict.values():
        rf_rel_name = rf.risk_factor_name
        rf_rel_name_id = rf.risk_factor_name_id
        rf_container_name = rf.container_name
        rf_tenor_token = rf.tenor_token

        # Get container meta
        rf_cm = config.container_meta_dict.get(rf_container_name)
        if rf_cm is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "risk_factors_dict references unknown container_name.\n"
                f"container_name={rf_container_name!r}\n"
                f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}"
            )

        # ======================================================== #
        # === HANDLE SCALAR CONTAINERS (FX SPOT, EQUITIES, ETC) == #
        # ======================================================== #
        if rf_cm.alias_type_name is None:
            if rf_tenor_token is not None:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Risk factor ID tenor_token ({rf_tenor_token}) must be None when alias_type_name is None.\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\n"
                    "container_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                )
            # Scalar factors just map to themselves (no term structure to roll)
            abs_to_rel_name_and_id[rf_rel_name] = (rf_rel_name, rf_rel_name_id)
            continue

        # Get alias schema
        rf_alias = config.alias_schema_dict.get(rf_cm.alias_type_name)
        if rf_alias is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "container_meta_dict references unknown alias_type_name.\n"
                f"container_name={rf_cm.container_name!r}\n"
                "alias_type_name={rf_cm.alias_type_name!r}"
            )

        # ================================ #
        # === RESOLVE TO ABSOLUTE NAME === #
        # ================================ #

        if rf_alias.alias_kind == "NONE":
            if rf_tenor_token is not None:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Risk factor ID tenor_token ({rf_tenor_token}) must be None for alias_kind = 'NONE'.\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                )
            abs_to_rel_name_and_id[rf_rel_name] = (rf_rel_name, rf_rel_name_id)
            continue

        elif rf_alias.alias_kind == "LABEL":
            if rf_tenor_token is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Risk factor missing tenor_token for alias_kind = 'LABEL'.\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                )
            label, signed_offset = parse_bracket_token(rf_tenor_token)
            if signed_offset is not None:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"alias_kind = 'LABEL' tenor_token ('{rf_tenor_token}') must not have +/- offset.\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                )
            rf_abs_name = f"{rf_container_name}_{label}"
            abs_to_rel_name_and_id[rf_abs_name] = (rf_rel_name, rf_rel_name_id)
            continue

        elif rf_alias.alias_kind == "OFFSET":
            if rf_tenor_token is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "alias_kind = OFFSET risk factor missing tenor_token.\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                )

            if (
                not rf_cm.spot_roll_rule_name
                or rf_cm.spot_roll_rule_name == "None"
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "alias_kind = OFFSET curve missing spot_roll_rule_name in container_meta.\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                )

            tenor_token_unit, tenor_token_signed_offset = parse_bracket_token(
                rf_tenor_token
            )
            if tenor_token_signed_offset is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "alias_kind = OFFSET tenor_token must have +/- integer.\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}\ntenor_token={rf_tenor_token!r}\n"
                )

            # Retrieve rule
            rf_srr = config.spot_roll_rules_dict.get(
                (rf_cm.spot_roll_rule_name, rf_cm.tenor_type_name)
            )
            if rf_srr is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Missing spot roll rule in spot_roll_rules_dict!\n"
                    f"spot_roll_rule_name={rf_cm.spot_roll_rule_name!r}\n"
                    f"tenor_type_name={rf_cm.tenor_type_name!r}\n"
                    f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                )

            rf_spot_roll_method = rf_srr.spot_roll_method
            rf_tenor_type_name = rf_cm.tenor_type_name

            rf_offset = int(rf_srr.offset_in_tenor_units or 0)
            rf_roll_month = int(rf_srr.roll_month or 0)
            rf_roll_day = int(rf_srr.roll_day or 0)
            rf_post_shift = int(rf_srr.post_roll_shift or 0)

            # Resolution Logic
            if (
                rf_tenor_type_name == "TENOR_YM"
                and rf_spot_roll_method == "CALENDAR_PERIOD"
            ):
                spot_month_yyyymm01 = add_months_to_date_and_return_yyyymm01(
                    d=date(as_of_date.year, as_of_date.month, 1),
                    months_to_add=rf_offset,
                )
                rf_abs_date_yyyymm01 = add_months_to_date_and_return_yyyymm01(
                    d=spot_month_yyyymm01,
                    months_to_add=int(tenor_token_signed_offset),
                )
                rf_abs_name = f"{rf_container_name}_{fmt_date_as_str_yyyymm(rf_abs_date_yyyymm01)}"
                abs_to_rel_name_and_id[rf_abs_name] = (
                    rf_rel_name,
                    rf_rel_name_id,
                )
                continue

            elif (
                rf_tenor_type_name == "TENOR_Y"
                and rf_spot_roll_method == "ROLL_ON_MMDD"
            ):
                spot_year = get_spot_year(
                    as_of_date=as_of_date,
                    roll_month=rf_roll_month,
                    roll_day=rf_roll_day,
                    post_roll_shift=rf_post_shift,
                )
                year = spot_year + int(tenor_token_signed_offset)
                rf_abs_name = f"{rf_container_name}_{year:04d}"
                abs_to_rel_name_and_id[rf_abs_name] = (
                    rf_rel_name,
                    rf_rel_name_id,
                )
                continue

            elif (
                rf_tenor_type_name == "TENOR_YMD"
                and rf_spot_roll_method == "HALF_YEAR_BY_DATE"
            ):
                fh_bh_ddmm = rf_srr.fh_bh_ddmm
                if not fh_bh_ddmm:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "spot_roll_method = 'HALF_YEAR_BY_DATE' missing fh_bh_ddmm!\n"
                        f"spot_roll_rule_name={rf_cm.spot_roll_rule_name!r}\n"
                        f"relative={rf_rel_name!r}\ncurve_name={rf_container_name!r}"
                    )

                fh_mmdd, bh_mmdd = fh_bh_ddmm.split("|")
                if len(fh_mmdd) != 4 or len(bh_mmdd) != 4:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"fh_bh_ddmm ('{fh_bh_ddmm}') must be of form 'ddmm|ddmm'!"
                    )

                as_of_year = as_of_date.year
                look_years = max(3, abs(int(tenor_token_signed_offset)) + 1)

                boundaries: list[date] = []
                for y in range(
                    as_of_year - look_years, as_of_year + look_years + 1
                ):
                    boundaries.append(
                        date(y, int(fh_mmdd[:2]), int(fh_mmdd[2:]))
                    )
                    boundaries.append(
                        date(y, int(bh_mmdd[:2]), int(bh_mmdd[2:]))
                    )
                boundaries.sort()

                idx_hy0 = bisect_left(boundaries, as_of_date)
                idx_target = idx_hy0 + int(tenor_token_signed_offset)

                if idx_target < 0 or idx_target >= len(boundaries):
                    raise ValueError(
                        "HALF_YEAR_BY_DATE offset out of range for constructed boundary grid.\n"
                        f"curve_name={rf_container_name!r}\nrelative={rf_rel_name!r}\n"
                    )

                abs_dt = boundaries[idx_target]
                yyyymmdd_str = (
                    f"{abs_dt.year:04d}{abs_dt.month:02d}{abs_dt.day:02d}"
                )
                rf_abs_name = f"{rf_container_name}_{yyyymmdd_str}"

                abs_to_rel_name_and_id[rf_abs_name] = (
                    rf_rel_name,
                    rf_rel_name_id,
                )
                continue

            # Inside your resolution block in resolution.py:

            elif (
                rf_tenor_type_name == "TENOR_HY_LABEL"
                and rf_spot_roll_method == "HALF_YEAR_BY_LABEL"
            ):
                # 1. Use the offset we already extracted via parse_bracket_token
                offset = int(tenor_token_signed_offset)

                # 2. Determine the "current" half-year based on the as-of date
                current_year = as_of_date.year
                # If month is 1-6, it's index 0 (FH). If 7-12, it's index 1 (BH).
                current_half_idx = 0 if as_of_date.month <= 6 else 1

                # 3. Calculate the absolute half-year mathematically
                total_half_years = (
                    (current_year * 2) + current_half_idx + offset
                )

                target_year = total_half_years // 2
                target_half_idx = total_half_years % 2

                # 4. Map the index back to your specific schema labels
                target_label = "FH" if target_half_idx == 0 else "BH"

                # 5. Construct the absolute name matching your abs_name_regex
                rf_abs_name = (
                    f"{rf_container_name}_{target_year:04d}_{target_label}"
                )

                # 6. Save it to the mapping dictionary so it isn't lost!
                abs_to_rel_name_and_id[rf_abs_name] = (
                    rf_rel_name,
                    rf_rel_name_id,
                )
                continue

            else:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Unsupported OFFSET resolution combination: "
                    f"(tenor_type_name, rf_spot_roll_method) = ('{rf_tenor_type_name}', '{rf_spot_roll_method}')\n"
                    f"curve_name={rf_container_name!r}\nrelative={rf_rel_name!r}\n"
                )

    return RiskFactorAbsToRelNameIdMap(
        as_of_date=as_of_date,
        abs_to_rel_name_and_id=abs_to_rel_name_and_id,
    )

```

### FILE: config\static_config\schema_objects.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/schema_objects.py
"""
Schema objects for config data.

Design intent:
- Immutable, slot-based value objects used by config loading/compiling/validation.
- Keep this module lightweight: avoid importing pandas/numpy here to prevent
  config schema from dragging heavy deps into runtime/model code.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
import re
from typing import Optional, Pattern, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.utils.printer import print_success_message

# =============== #
# --- LOOKUPS --- #
# =============== #


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Ccy:
    ccy: str
    ccy_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AssetClass:
    asset_class: str
    asset_class_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AssetSubclass:
    asset_subclass: str
    asset_subclass_id: int
    asset_class: str  # FK -> AssetClass.asset_class (validated in compile step)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class UnitType:
    unit_type_name: str
    unit_type_name_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class UnitScale:
    unit_scale_name: str
    unit_scale_name_id: int
    scale_to_base: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ShiftType:
    shift_type: str
    shift_type_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class InterpolationMethod:
    interpolation_method: str
    interpolation_method_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExtrapolationMethod:
    extrapolation_method: str
    extrapolation_method_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SurfaceYAxisType:
    y_axis_type_name: str


# =================== #
# --- CONVENTIONS --- #
# =================== #


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DayCountConvention:
    day_count_convention: str
    day_count_convention_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BusinessDayConvention:
    business_day_convention: str
    business_day_convention_id: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CompoundingConvention:
    compounding_convention: str
    compounding_convention_id: int
    compounding_type: str
    frequency_per_year: Optional[float]


# ===================== #
# --- CORE ENTITIES --- #
# ===================== #


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ContainerMeta:
    """
    Metadata for a container (curve, surface, FX spot, etc).

    Notes:
    - tenor_type_name / spot_roll_rule_name / alias_type_name are typically None for container_dim == 0 (e.g., FX).
    - surface_y_axis_type_name / interp_method_name_y / extrap_method_name_y are typically None for non-surfaces.
    """

    container_name: str
    container_name_id: int

    base_ccy: str
    quote_ccy: str

    unit_type_name: str
    unit_scale_name: str

    container_dim: int  # 0 or 1 for now (extend later)

    asset_class: str
    asset_subclass: str

    tenor_type_name: Optional[str]
    spot_roll_rule_name: Optional[str]
    alias_type_name: Optional[str]

    surface_y_axis_type_name: Optional[str]

    shift_type_name: str

    interp_method_name_x: Optional[str]
    interp_method_name_y: Optional[str]
    extrap_method_name_x: Optional[str]
    extrap_method_name_y: Optional[str]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.container_dim not in (0, 1):
            raise ValueError(
                f"container_dim must be 0 or 1 for now; got {self.container_dim!r} "
                f"for container_name={self.container_name!r}"
            )

        # Basic "dim implies curve metadata" checks.
        if self.container_dim == 0:
            # Scalar containers (FX spot etc) should not specify tenor/roll/alias.
            if self.tenor_type_name is not None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "tenor_type_name must be None when container_dim==0 "
                    f"(container_name={self.container_name!r}, tenor_type_name={self.tenor_type_name!r})"
                )
            if self.spot_roll_rule_name is not None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "spot_roll_rule_name must be None when container_dim==0 "
                    f"(container_name={self.container_name!r}, spot_roll_rule_name={self.spot_roll_rule_name!r})"
                )
            if self.alias_type_name is not None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "alias_type_name must be None when container_dim==0 "
                    f"(container_name={self.container_name!r}, alias_type_name={self.alias_type_name!r})"
                )

        # Strong defaults that catch config typos early.
        # (Hard errors only for the really obvious ones.)
        if self.asset_class == "FX":
            if self.container_dim != 0:
                raise ValueError(
                    "FX containers must have container_dim==0 "
                    f"(container_name={self.container_name!r}, container_dim={self.container_dim!r})"
                )
            if self.unit_type_name != "FX_RATE":
                raise ValueError(
                    "FX containers must have unit_type_name=='FX_RATE' "
                    f"(container_name={self.container_name!r}, unit_type_name={self.unit_type_name!r})"
                )

        if self.asset_class == "IR":
            # IR should never be FX_RATE; this catches copy/paste errors.
            if self.unit_type_name == "FX_RATE":
                raise ValueError(
                    "IR containers cannot have unit_type_name=='FX_RATE' "
                    f"(container_name={self.container_name!r})"
                )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RiskFactor:
    risk_factor_name: str
    risk_factor_name_id: int
    tenor_token: Optional[str]
    container_name: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        # Remove tenor token as a suffix only (safer than replace)
        rf_name_prefix = (
            self.risk_factor_name.removesuffix(self.tenor_token)
            if self.tenor_token
            else self.risk_factor_name
        )

        if rf_name_prefix != self.container_name:
            raise ValueError(
                f"risk_factor_name ({self.risk_factor_name!r}) must match "
                f"container_name ({self.container_name!r}) "
                f"after removing tenor_token ({self.tenor_token!r})."
            )


# ===================== #
# --- SCHEMA TABLES --- #
# ===================== #


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TenorSchema:
    tenor_type_name: str
    tenor_unit: str
    digits: int
    token_regex: Optional[Pattern[str]]
    abs_name_regex: Optional[Pattern[str]]
    rel_name_regex: Optional[Pattern[str]]
    abs_name_example: str
    rel_name_example: str

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        """Returns a human-readable, formatted tree of the schema rules."""
        # Helper to safely extract the raw string pattern from the compiled regex
        fmt_re = lambda r: f"'{r.pattern}'" if r else "None"

        return (
            f"TenorSchema('{self.tenor_type_name}')\n"
            f"  ├─ Tenor Unit: {self.tenor_unit} (Digits: {self.digits})\n"
            f"  ├─ Regex Rules:\n"
            f"  │   ├─ Token:    {fmt_re(self.token_regex)}\n"
            f"  │   ├─ Absolute: {fmt_re(self.abs_name_regex)}\n"
            f"  │   └─ Relative: {fmt_re(self.rel_name_regex)}\n"
            f"  └─ Examples:\n"
            f"      ├─ Absolute: '{self.abs_name_example}'\n"
            f"      └─ Relative: '{self.rel_name_example}'"
        )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        """Fallback to the pretty string when printing from a list or dict."""
        return self.__str__()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AliasSchema:
    alias_type_name: str
    alias_kind: str  # OFFSET or LABEL
    alias_unit: Optional[str]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SpotRollRule:
    spot_roll_rule_name: str
    tenor_type_name: str
    spot_roll_method: str
    offset_in_tenor_units: Optional[int]
    roll_month: Optional[int]
    roll_day: Optional[int]
    fh_bh_ddmm: Optional[str]
    post_roll_shift: Optional[int]


# ======================== #
# --- HOLIDAY CALENDAR --- #
# ======================== #


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class HolidayCalendar:
    holiday_calendar_name: str
    holiday_dates: Optional[Tuple[date, ...]]

```

### FILE: config\static_config\static_config.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/configs/static_config.py
"""
Runtime configuration containers.
Strictly native Python structures (no Pandas/DataFrames) for maximum efficiency.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np

from riskquant.config.static_config.schema_objects import (
    AliasSchema,
    AssetClass,
    AssetSubclass,
    BusinessDayConvention,
    Ccy,
    CompoundingConvention,
    ContainerMeta,
    DayCountConvention,
    ExtrapolationMethod,
    HolidayCalendar,
    InterpolationMethod,
    RiskFactor,
    ShiftType,
    SpotRollRule,
    SurfaceYAxisType,
    TenorSchema,
    UnitScale,
    UnitType,
)
from riskquant.utils.tenors import parse_bracket_token


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RiskStaticConfig:
    """
    Container for risk static configuration tables for runtime use.
    No pandas DataFrames here - just pure Python structures for maximum efficiency.
    """

    # Core
    container_meta_dict: Dict[str, ContainerMeta] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    risk_factors_dict: Dict[str, RiskFactor] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )

    # Schemas & Rules
    tenor_schema_dict: Dict[str, TenorSchema] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    alias_schema_dict: Dict[str, AliasSchema] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    spot_roll_rules_dict: Dict[Tuple[str, str], SpotRollRule] = field(
        init=True, repr=False
    )
    surface_y_axis_schema_dict: Dict[str, SurfaceYAxisType] = field(
        init=True, repr=False
    )

    # Lookups
    asset_classes_dict: Dict[str, AssetClass] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    asset_subclasses_dict: Dict[str, AssetSubclass] = field(
        init=True, repr=False
    )
    unit_types_dict: Dict[str, UnitType] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    unit_scales_dict: Dict[str, UnitScale] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    day_count_conventions_dict: Dict[str, DayCountConvention] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    business_day_conventions_dict: Dict[str, BusinessDayConvention] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    compounding_conventions_dict: Dict[str, CompoundingConvention] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    interpolation_methods_dict: Dict[str, InterpolationMethod] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    extrapolation_methods_dict: Dict[str, ExtrapolationMethod] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    ccy_dict: Dict[str, Ccy] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )
    shift_types_dict: Dict[str, ShiftType] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )

    # Calendars
    holiday_calendars_dict: Dict[str, HolidayCalendar] = field(
        init=True,
        repr=False,
    )

    # --- INTERNAL CACHES --- #
    # Initialized as None; populated lazily on first access using object.__setattr__
    _container_name_to_container_id: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )
    _rf_name_to_rf_id: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )
    _rf_id_to_risk_factor: Optional[Dict[int, RiskFactor]] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )
    _rf_id_to_shift_type_id: Optional[Dict[int, int]] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )
    _curve_tenor_index: Optional[
        Dict[str, Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, Dict[str, int]]]
    ] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )
    _rf_id_to_ccy: Optional[Dict[int, str]] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )
    _container_id_to_ccy: Optional[Dict[int, str]] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )
    _container_name_to_y_axis_type_name: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
        init=False,
    )

    @property
    def num_risk_factors(self) -> int:
        return len(self.risk_factors_dict)

    @property
    def num_containers(self) -> int:
        return len(self.container_meta_dict)

    @property
    def max_risk_factor_id(self) -> int:
        """The maximum risk_factor_name_id across all risk factors. Useful for sizing arrays."""
        return max(
            rf.risk_factor_name_id for rf in self.risk_factors_dict.values()
        )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.container_meta_dict:
            raise ValueError(
                "container_meta_dict is empty. Check your container_meta sheet and 'active' flags."
            )
        if not self.risk_factors_dict:
            raise ValueError(
                "risk_factors_dict is empty. Check your risk_factors sheet and 'active' flags."
            )

    def summary(self) -> str:
        lines = [
            "=== Risk Static Config Summary ===",
            f"Containers Mapped: {len(self.container_meta_dict)}",
            f"Risk Factors: {len(self.risk_factors_dict)}",
            f"Asset Classes: {len(self.asset_classes_dict)}",
            f"Currencies: {len(self.ccy_dict)}",
            f"Shift Types: {len(self.shift_types_dict)}",
            "==================================",
        ]
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def get_risk_factor_id(self, risk_factor_name: str) -> int:
        return self.risk_factors_dict[risk_factor_name].risk_factor_name_id

    @property
    def container_name_to_container_id(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """A build-once cache: container_name -> container_id"""
        if self._container_name_to_container_id is None:
            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "_container_name_to_container_id",
                {
                    k: v.container_name_id
                    for k, v in self.container_meta_dict.items()
                },
            )
        return self._container_name_to_container_id

    @property
    def rf_name_to_rf_id(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """A build-once cache: risk_factor_name -> risk_factor_name_id"""
        if self._rf_name_to_rf_id is None:
            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "_rf_name_to_rf_id",
                {
                    k: v.risk_factor_name_id
                    for k, v in self.risk_factors_dict.items()
                },
            )
        return self._rf_name_to_rf_id

    # @property
    # def rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id(self) -> Dict[int, Tuple[int, float]]:
    #     """A build-once cache of RF ID-level proxy overrides: required_rf_id -> (proxy_rf_id, vol_scale)"""
    #     if self._rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id is None:
    #         rf_name_map = self.rf_name_to_rf_id
    #         out: Dict[int, Tuple[int, float]] = {}
    #         for p in self.risk_factor_proxies_dict.values():
    #             rf_id = int(rf_name_map[p.risk_factor_name])
    #             proxy_rf_id = int(rf_name_map[p.proxy_risk_factor_name])
    #             scale = float(p.proxy_risk_factor_vol_scaling_factor or 1.0)
    #             out[rf_id] = (proxy_rf_id, scale)
    #         object.__setattr__(self, "_rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id", out)
    #     return self._rf_id_to_proxy_rf_id

    @property
    def rf_id_to_risk_factor(self) -> Dict[int, RiskFactor]:
        """Cached mapping: risk_factor_name_id (int) -> RiskFactor"""
        if self._rf_id_to_risk_factor is None:
            out: Dict[int, RiskFactor] = {}
            for rf in self.risk_factors_dict.values():
                rf_id = int(rf.risk_factor_name_id)
                if rf_id in out:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Duplicate risk_factor_name_id detected: {rf_id}"
                    )
                out[rf_id] = rf
            object.__setattr__(self, "_rf_id_to_risk_factor", out)
        return self._rf_id_to_risk_factor

    @property
    def rf_id_to_shift_type_id(self) -> Dict[int, int]:
        """Cached mapping: risk_factor_name_id (int) -> shift_type_id (int)"""
        if self._rf_id_to_shift_type_id is None:
            out: Dict[int, int] = {}
            for rf in self.risk_factors_dict.values():
                rf_id = int(rf.risk_factor_name_id)
                cm = self.container_meta_dict.get(rf.container_name)
                if cm is None:
                    raise KeyError(
                        f"curve_name={rf.container_name!r} not found in container_meta_dict."
                    )

                st_obj = self.shift_types_dict.get(cm.shift_type_name)
                if st_obj is None:
                    raise KeyError(
                        f"shift_type_name={cm.shift_type_name!r} not found in shift_types_dict."
                    )

                out[rf_id] = int(st_obj.shift_type_id)
            object.__setattr__(self, "_rf_id_to_shift_type_id", out)
        return self._rf_id_to_shift_type_id

    @property
    def curve_tenor_index(
        self,
    ) -> Dict[str, Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, Dict[str, int]]]:
        """Cached per-curve tenor lookup used by curve-level proxy resolution."""
        if self._curve_tenor_index is not None:
            return self._curve_tenor_index

        offsets: Dict[str, list[int]] = {}
        offset_ids: Dict[str, list[int]] = {}
        labels: Dict[str, Dict[str, int]] = {}

        for rf_id, rf in self.rf_id_to_risk_factor.items():
            rf_curve_name = rf.container_name
            rf_tenor_token = rf.tenor_token

            if rf_tenor_token is None:
                labels.setdefault(rf_curve_name, {})[""] = int(rf_id)
                continue

            rf_unit, rf_offset = parse_bracket_token(rf_tenor_token)

            if rf_offset is None:
                labels.setdefault(rf_curve_name, {})[rf_unit] = int(rf_id)
            else:
                offsets.setdefault(rf_curve_name, []).append(int(rf_offset))
                offset_ids.setdefault(rf_curve_name, []).append(int(rf_id))

        out: Dict[str, Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, Dict[str, int]]] = {}

        for rf_curve_name, rf_offset_list in offsets.items():
            rf_ids_list = offset_ids[rf_curve_name]
            rf_offsets_arr = np.asarray(rf_offset_list, dtype=np.int32)
            rf_ids_arr = np.asarray(rf_ids_list, dtype=np.int32)

            rf_offset_idx = np.argsort(rf_offsets_arr, kind="mergesort")

            out[rf_curve_name] = (
                rf_offsets_arr[rf_offset_idx],
                rf_ids_arr[rf_offset_idx],
                labels.get(rf_curve_name, {}),
            )

        for rf_curve_name, rf_curve_label_to_rf_id in labels.items():
            if rf_curve_name not in out:
                out[rf_curve_name] = (
                    np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32),
                    np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32),
                    rf_curve_label_to_rf_id,
                )

        object.__setattr__(self, "_curve_tenor_index", out)
        return out

    @property
    def rf_id_to_ccy(self) -> Dict[int, str]:
        """A build-once cache mapping both risk factor IDs to their quote currency."""
        if self._rf_id_to_ccy is None:
            out: Dict[int, str] = {}

            # Map all Risk Factor Names to their parent container's quote_ccy
            for _, rf in self.risk_factors_dict.items():
                parent_container_meta = self.container_meta_dict[
                    rf.container_name
                ]
                out[rf.risk_factor_name_id] = parent_container_meta.quote_ccy

            object.__setattr__(self, "_rf_id_to_ccy", out)

        return self._rf_id_to_ccy

    @property
    def container_id_to_ccy(self) -> Dict[int, str]:
        """A build-once cache mapping both container IDs to their quote currency."""
        if self._container_id_to_ccy is None:
            out: Dict[int, str] = {}

            # Map all Container Names directly to their quote_ccy
            for _, c_meta in self.container_meta_dict.items():
                out[c_meta.container_name_id] = c_meta.quote_ccy

            object.__setattr__(self, "_container_id_to_ccy", out)

        return self._container_id_to_ccy

    @property
    def container_name_to_y_axis_type_name(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """A build-once cache mapping container names to their Y-Axis type names."""
        if self._container_name_to_y_axis_type_name is None:
            out: Dict[str, str] = {}
            for curve_name, c_meta in self.container_meta_dict.items():
                if c_meta.surface_y_axis_type_name is not None:
                    out[curve_name] = c_meta.surface_y_axis_type_name

            # Bypass immutability for the lazy cache
            object.__setattr__(self, "_container_name_to_y_axis_type_name", out)

        return self._container_name_to_y_axis_type_name

```

### FILE: config\static_config\validate.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/config/validate.py
"""
High-performance vectorized validation for risk static config data.

Design goals:
  - Fail-fast before memory allocation of runtime objects.
  - Zero native Python loops over dataframe rows.
  - Utilize NumPy/Pandas C-level vectorization for constraints (Unique, FK).
"""

from typing import Dict

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from riskquant.market_data.curves.extrap_methods import EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_methods import INTERP_METHOD_NAMES


def _validate_risk_factor_tokens(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Validates that every tenor_token in the risk_factors sheet strictly conforms
    to the token_regex defined for its specific tenor_type_name in the schema.
    """
    rf_df = dict_of_dfs.get("risk_factors")
    cm_df = dict_of_dfs.get("container_meta")
    ts_df = dict_of_dfs.get("tenor_schema")

    if any(df is None or df.empty for df in (rf_df, cm_df, ts_df)):
        return

    # 1. Filter out scalars/FX spot (which legitimately have no tenor_token)
    tokens_df = rf_df[rf_df["tenor_token"].notna()].copy()
    if tokens_df.empty:
        return

    # 2. Join the hierarchy to figure out which regex each token SHOULD use
    # container_name -> tenor_type_name
    cm_mapping = cm_df.set_index("container_name")["tenor_type_name"]
    tokens_df["tenor_type_name"] = tokens_df["container_name"].map(cm_mapping)

    # tenor_type_name -> token_regex
    ts_mapping = ts_df.set_index("tenor_type_name")["token_regex"]
    tokens_df["expected_regex"] = tokens_df["tenor_type_name"].map(ts_mapping)

    # print(tokens_df.to_string())

    # 3. Vectorized validation using groupby
    # We group by the regex pattern so we can use Pandas' fast vectorized C-level string matching
    valid_mask = pd.Series(False, index=tokens_df.index)

    for regex_pattern, group in tokens_df.groupby(
        "expected_regex", dropna=False
    ):
        if pd.isna(regex_pattern):
            # If a container maps to a tenor_type_name that has no regex, mark as invalid
            continue

        # Apply the regex to the entire group at once
        valid_mask.loc[group.index] = (
            group["tenor_token"].astype(str).str.match(str(regex_pattern))
        )

    # 4. Catch violators and fail fast
    if not valid_mask.all():
        violators_df = tokens_df[~valid_mask]

        # # Format a clean error message showing exactly what failed
        # sample = (
        #     violators_df[
        #         [
        #             "risk_factor_name",
        #             "tenor_token",
        #             "tenor_type_name",
        #             "expected_regex",
        #         ]
        #     ]
        #     .to_dict("records")
        # )

        raise ValueError(
            f"Config Validation Failed: Found risk factors with invalid tenor_tokens.\n"
            f"Tokens must strictly match the token_regex defined in the tenor_schema.\n"
            f"Sample violators:\n"
            f"{violators_df.to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_container_meta(dict_of_dfs):
    # Extract the curve schema dataframe
    container_meta_df = dict_of_dfs.get("container_meta")

    # If empty.
    if container_meta_df is None or container_meta_df.empty:
        return

    # ---> INTERP METHODS <--- #

    required_cols = ["interp_method_name_x", "interp_method_name_y"]
    missing_cols = [
        c for c in required_cols if c not in container_meta_df.columns
    ]
    if missing_cols:
        raise ValueError(
            f"container_meta missing required columns: {missing_cols}"
        )

    # Normalise
    interp_df = (
        container_meta_df[required_cols]
        .astype(str)
        .apply(lambda col: col.str.strip().str.upper())
    )

    # Flatten unique values across both axes
    unique_methods = set(interp_df.values.ravel())

    # Remove blanks / null-like artifacts
    unique_methods = {
        m
        for m in unique_methods
        if m
        not in {
            "",
            "NONE",
            "NAN",
            "NA",
            "N/A",
            "<NA>",
            "NULL",
        }
    }

    # Define allowed methods (ideally centralised elsewhere)
    VALID_INTERP_METHODS = set(INTERP_METHOD_NAMES)

    # Validate
    invalid_methods = unique_methods - VALID_INTERP_METHODS
    if invalid_methods:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid interpolation methods found: {sorted(invalid_methods)}. "
            f"Allowed methods: {sorted(VALID_INTERP_METHODS)}"
        )

    # ---> EXTRAP METHODS <--- #

    required_cols = ["extrap_method_name_x", "extrap_method_name_y"]
    missing_cols = [
        c for c in required_cols if c not in container_meta_df.columns
    ]
    if missing_cols:
        raise ValueError(
            f"container_meta missing required columns: {missing_cols}"
        )

    # Normalise
    extrap_df = (
        container_meta_df[required_cols]
        .astype(str)
        .apply(lambda col: col.str.strip().str.upper())
    )

    # Flatten unique values across both axes
    unique_methods = set(extrap_df.values.ravel())

    # Remove blanks / null-like artifacts
    unique_methods = {
        m
        for m in unique_methods
        if m
        not in {
            "",
            "NONE",
            "NAN",
            "NA",
            "N/A",
            "<NA>",
            "NULL",
        }
    }

    # Define allowed methods (ideally centralised elsewhere)
    VALID_EXTRAP_METHODS = set(EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES)

    # Validate
    invalid_methods = unique_methods - VALID_EXTRAP_METHODS
    if invalid_methods:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid extrapolation methods found: {sorted(invalid_methods)}. "
            f"Allowed methods: {sorted(VALID_EXTRAP_METHODS)}"
        )

    return


def validate_static_config_dfs(dict_of_dfs: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Executes vectorized schema checks (uniqueness and foreign keys) across the
    raw configuration dataframes.

    Args:
        dict_of_dfs: The raw loaded dataframes mapped by sheet name.

    Raises:
        ValueError: On the first violation of schema constraints.
    """

    # --------------------------------------------------------- #
    # 1. UNIQUENESS CONSTRAINTS (Primary Keys / Alt Keys)
    # --------------------------------------------------------- #
    # Structure: sheet_name -> tuple of column names that must be unique
    unique_constraints = {
        "container_meta": ("container_name", "container_name_id"),
        "risk_factors": ("risk_factor_name", "risk_factor_name_id"),
        "asset_classes": ("asset_class", "asset_class_id"),
        "asset_subclasses": ("asset_subclass", "asset_subclass_id"),
        "unit_types": ("unit_type_name", "unit_type_name_id"),
        "unit_scales": ("unit_scale_name", "unit_scale_name_id"),
        "ccy": ("ccy", "ccy_id"),
        "shift_types": ("shift_type", "shift_type_id"),
        # "container_proxies": ("container_name",),
        # "risk_factor_proxies": ("risk_factor_name",),
    }

    for sheet_name, cols in unique_constraints.items():
        df = dict_of_dfs.get(sheet_name)
        if df is None or df.empty:
            continue

        for col in cols:
            if col not in df.columns:
                continue  # Missing columns handled separately if strictly required

            # .is_unique is an O(N) C-level check.
            if not df[col].is_unique:
                # Only pay the cost of finding the exact duplicates if we fail
                duplicates = df[col][df[col].duplicated()].unique()
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Validation failed: '{sheet_name}.{col}' must be unique. "
                    f"Duplicates found: {duplicates.tolist()}"
                )

    # --------------------------------------------------------- #
    # 2. FOREIGN KEY CONSTRAINTS (Referential Integrity)
    # --------------------------------------------------------- #
    # Structure: tuple(child_sheet, child_col) -> tuple(parent_sheet, parent_col)
    fk_constraints = {
        ("risk_factors", "container_name"): (
            "container_meta",
            "container_name",
        ),
        ("asset_subclasses", "asset_class"): ("asset_classes", "asset_class"),
    }

    for (c_sheet, c_col), (p_sheet, p_col) in fk_constraints.items():
        c_df = dict_of_dfs.get(c_sheet)
        p_df = dict_of_dfs.get(p_sheet)

        if (
            c_df is None
            or p_df is None
            or c_col not in c_df.columns
            or p_col not in p_df.columns
        ):
            continue

        # Extract unique arrays directly. Dropping NA ensures we don't flag optional/blank FKs.
        child_vals = c_df[c_col].dropna().unique()
        parent_vals = p_df[p_col].dropna().unique()

        # np.setdiff1d is a highly optimized C-level array operation.
        # It is significantly faster and uses less memory than Python set(A) - set(B).
        missing_fks = np.setdiff1d(child_vals, parent_vals, assume_unique=True)

        if missing_fks.size > 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Validation failed: '{c_sheet}.{c_col}' contains values not present in "
                f"'{p_sheet}.{p_col}'. Missing: {missing_fks.tolist()}"
            )

    # --------------------------------------------------------- #
    # 3. DOMAIN CONSTRAINTS (Syntax & Business Logic)
    # --------------------------------------------------------- #
    # Now that we know containers and schemas exist and map correctly,
    # we enforce the strict data-driven regex rules on the inputs.

    _validate_risk_factor_tokens(dict_of_dfs)

    # ----------------------------------------- #
    # 4. VALIDATE THE INTERPOLATION/EXTRAPOLATION METHODS IN THE CURVE SCHEMA
    # ----------------------------------------- #

    # Validate.
    _validate_container_meta(dict_of_dfs)

    # You can add future domain checks here
    # (e.g., validate_shift_types, validate_currency_codes)

```

# src/riskquant/constants.py

"""
Common constants used across the riskquant codebase.

This module separates:
- typed sentinel values used in numeric payloads / arrays
- general configuration constants used by the portfolio engine
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import date

import numpy as np


# ==============================================================================
# Typed sentinel values for payload fields
# ==============================================================================
# These must stay numpy scalars where dtype compatibility matters.

# Sentinel for "no valid ID" in any of the ID fields (instrument, universe index, risk factor, curve).
NO_UIX = np.int32(-1)
NO_PRICE_ID = np.int32(-2)
NO_PRICE_CURVE_ID = np.int32(-3)
NO_VOL_ID = np.int32(-4)

# Sentinel for "no discount curve" in the discount curve linkage fields.
NO_DISCOUNT_CURVE_ID = np.int32(-5)

# No FX rate required (e.g. for SPOT trades where the underlying and settlement currencies are the same).
NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED = np.int32(-6)

# Sentinels for missing dates in the date fields (settlement, delivery, expiry).
NO_DATE_UIX = np.int32(-7)
NO_DELIVERY_DATE_ORDINAL32 = np.int32(-8)
NO_SETTLEMENT_DATE_ORDINAL32 = np.int32(-9)
NO_EXPIRY_DATE_ORDINAL32 = np.int32(-10)

# Sentinel for "not an option" in the is_call field.
NOT_OPTION = np.int8(-11)


# ==============================================================================
# Date encoding
# ==============================================================================

DATE_EPOCH: date = date(1970, 1, 1)


# ==============================================================================
# Numeric configuration
# ==============================================================================

ROUND_DECIMALS_LEVEL = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_QUANTITY = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_STRIKE_PRICE = 8

POSITION_DUST_TOLERANCE = 1e-8

# ==========================================
# --- NAMING CONVENTIONS
# ==========================================

# Prefix expected by the engine to map an underlying asset to its volatility surface/node
VOL_RISK_FACTOR_PREFIX: str = "vol_"


# ============================================
# --- MOCK / FALLBACK VALUES
# ============================================

# Used as a fallback in the vanilla_european_option_curve pricer before vol surfaces are built out.
MOCK_VOLATILITY: float = 0.50


# ==============================================================================
# --- EWMA Volatility Calculation Constants
# ==============================================================================

# EWMA volatility decay factor (lambda) used in the FHS volatility updating, following RiskMetrics standard.
EWMA_DECAY_FACTOR: float = 0.94

# Maximum allowed volatility ratio to prevent extreme scaling in the FHS Z-score calculation.
MAX_EWMA_VOL_RATIO: float = 10.0

# The minimum allowed variance to prevent division by zero or extremely large Z-scores in the FHS calculation.
MIN_EWMA_VARIANCE: float = 1e-12

```

# riskquant/exceptions.py


class PrettyKeyError(KeyError):
    """
    KeyError variant that prints the message using str(...) instead of repr(...),
    allowing multi-line, human-readable diagnostics while preserving KeyError
    semantics.
    """

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return str(self.args[0])

```
