```python
#                     cum_shock += r_log * col_vols[t1]
#                 out_col_vals[b] = cum_shock

#         elif st == SHIFT_SIMPLE_RETURN:
#             for b in range(B):
#                 cum_ret = 1.0
#                 path_start = base_t + b
#                 for step in range(h):
#                     t1 = path_start + step
#                     t0 = t1 - 1
#                     v1 = col_vals[t1]
#                     v0 = col_vals[t0]
#                     r_simple = (v1 / v0) - 1.0
#                     cum_ret *= 1.0 + (r_simple * col_vols[t1])
#                 out_col_vals[b] = cum_ret - 1.0

#         elif st == SHIFT_DIFFERENCE:
#             for b in range(B):
#                 cum_diff = 0.0
#                 path_start = base_t + b
#                 for step in range(h):
#                     t1 = path_start + step
#                     t0 = t1 - 1
#                     v1 = col_vals[t1]
#                     v0 = col_vals[t0]
#                     r_diff = v1 - v0
#                     cum_diff += r_diff * col_vols[t1]
#                 out_col_vals[b] = cum_diff


@nb.njit(parallel=True, fastmath=True, cache=True)
def _kernel_fused_filtered_h_day_shock(
    *,
    residuals: np.ndarray,  # Fortran-contiguous (T x K_req)
    vol_ratios: np.ndarray,  # Fortran-contiguous (T x K_req)
    cols: np.ndarray,  # K_req, column indices into original values (for reference only, not used in calculations)
    shift_types: np.ndarray,
    inv_perm: np.ndarray,
    t_start: int,
    h: int,
    B: int,
    out: np.ndarray,  # Fortran-contiguous (B x K_req)
):
    K = cols.shape[0]

    for k in nb.prange(K):
        st = shift_types[k]
        out_col = inv_perm[k]

        # Read directly from the aligned K_req arrays
        col_res = residuals[:, k]
        col_vols = vol_ratios[:, k]
        out_col_vals = out[:, out_col]

        base_t = t_start - h + 1

        if st == SHIFT_LOG_RETURN:
            for b in range(B):
                cum_shock = 0.0
                path_start = base_t + b
                for step in range(h):
                    t1 = path_start + step
                    # Scale the clean residual, not the raw return
                    cum_shock += col_res[t1] * col_vols[t1]
                out_col_vals[b] = cum_shock

        elif st == SHIFT_SIMPLE_RETURN:
            for b in range(B):
                cum_ret = 1.0
                path_start = base_t + b
                for step in range(h):
                    t1 = path_start + step
                    # Scale the clean residual, then compound
                    cum_ret *= 1.0 + (col_res[t1] * col_vols[t1])
                out_col_vals[b] = cum_ret - 1.0

        elif st == SHIFT_DIFFERENCE:
            for b in range(B):
                cum_diff = 0.0
                path_start = base_t + b
                for step in range(h):
                    t1 = path_start + step
                    # Scale the clean residual
                    cum_diff += col_res[t1] * col_vols[t1]
                out_col_vals[b] = cum_diff


# =========================================================================
# FILTERED HISTORICAL SIMULATION (FHS) GENERATOR
# =========================================================================


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class FilteredHistoricalScenarioGenerator(ScenarioGenerator):
    """
    Generates scenarios via Filtered Historical Simulation (FHS).

    Constructs an h-day synthetic path by applying Exponentially Weighted
    Moving Average (EWMA) volatility scaling to historical returns. This ensures
    that historical shocks are standardized to the current market volatility
    environment before aggregation.

    References
    ----------
    Hull, J., & White, A. (1998). Incorporating volatility updating into the historical simulation method for value-at-risk. Journal of Risk, 1(1), 5-19.
    https://doi.org/10.21314/JOR.1998.001

    Barone-Adesi, G., Bourgoin, F., & Giannopoulos, K. (1998). Don't look back. Risk, 11(8), 100-103.
    (Pioneered the 'Filtered Historical Simulation' terminology and empirical application of scaled residuals).

    J.P. Morgan / Reuters. (1996). RiskMetrics Technical Document. 4th Edition. New York.
    (Industry standard foundation for EWMA volatility tracking and the default lambda=0.94 decay factor).
    """

    matrix: RiskFactorMatrix  # MUST be Fortran-contiguous
    horizon: int = 1
    decay_factor: float = EWMA_DECAY_FACTOR  # Standard RiskMetrics lambda
    max_vol_ratio: float = MAX_EWMA_VOL_RATIO
    shock_dtype: type[np.float32] | type[np.float64] = np.float32

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.matrix.values.shape[0] < 3:
            raise ValueError("History too short for EWMA calculation.")

    def iter_batches(
        self,
        *,
        required_shift_type_id: np.ndarray,
        source_factor_ids: np.ndarray,
        shock_scale: np.ndarray,
        batch_size: int,
    ) -> Iterator[ScenarioSetBatch]:

        T = self.matrix.values.shape[0]
        h = int(self.horizon)
        S_total = T - h

        id_to_col = build_factor_id_to_col_index(self.matrix.factor_ids)

        shock_universe = build_shock_universe(
            required_shift_type_id=required_shift_type_id,
            source_factor_ids=source_factor_ids,
            id_to_col=id_to_col,
        )

        K_req = shock_universe.compute_plan.cols_sorted.shape[0]

        # --- 0. RESIDUALIZE RETURNS VIA JIT-COMPILED AR(1) FILTER ---

        # Get the AR(1) residuals for the entire history and all required columns in a single JIT-compiled pass.
        # Only apply A1(1) to auto-correlated factors - only de-mean non-auto-correlated factors.
        ar1_residuals = np.empty((T, K_req), dtype=np.float64, order="F")

        kernel_ar1_residuals(
            values=self.matrix.values,
            cols=shock_universe.compute_plan.cols_sorted,
            shift_types=shock_universe.compute_plan.shift_types_sorted,
            out_residuals=ar1_residuals,
        )

        # --- 1. JIT COMPILE & EXECUTE EWMA SCALARS ON THE FLY ---
        # We calculate the FHS scalars specifically for the exact shift-type mappings
        # requested by the universe, storing them in a tight (T x K_req) Fortran matrix.
        vol_ratios = np.empty((T, K_req), dtype=np.float64, order="F")

        kernel_ewma_vol_ratios_from_residuals(
            residuals=ar1_residuals,
            decay_factor=self.decay_factor,
            max_vol_ratio=self.max_vol_ratio,
            out_ratios=vol_ratios,
        )

        # --- 2. STREAM SCENARIOS ---
        scenario_offset = 0
        t_start = h

        while t_start < T:
            t_end = min(T, t_start + int(batch_size))
            B = t_end - t_start

            shocks = np.empty((B, K_req), dtype=self.shock_dtype, order="F")

            _kernel_fused_filtered_h_day_shock(
                residuals=ar1_residuals,
                vol_ratios=vol_ratios,
                cols=shock_universe.compute_plan.cols_sorted,
                shift_types=shock_universe.compute_plan.shift_types_sorted,
                inv_perm=shock_universe.compute_plan.inverse_permutation,
                t_start=t_start,
                h=h,
                B=B,
                out=shocks,
            )

            yield ScenarioSetBatch(
                shocks=shocks,
                required_to_shock_uix=shock_universe.required_to_shock_uix,
                shock_scale=shock_scale,
                required_shift_type_id=required_shift_type_id,
                scenario_offset=scenario_offset,
                scenario_count_total=S_total,
            )

            scenario_offset += B
            t_start = t_end

```

### FILE: simlab\scenarios\parametric.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/simlab/scenarios/parametric.py
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Iterator, Literal

import numpy as np

from riskquant.simlab.scenarios.base import (
    ScenarioGenerator,
    ScenarioSetBatch,
    build_shock_key_plan,
)

DistributionKind = Literal[
    "standard_normal",
    "student_t",
    "laplace",
    "variance_gamma",
    "regime_switching_gmm",
]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class ParametricMCScenarioGenerator(ScenarioGenerator):
    """
    Parametric shock-space scenario generator strictly supporting Gaussian Scale Mixtures.
    """

    factor_ids: np.ndarray
    cholesky_matrix: np.ndarray
    volatilities: np.ndarray
    total_scenarios: int
    distribution_kind: DistributionKind
    seed: int | None = None
    student_t_df: float | None = None
    vg_nu: float | None = None
    prob_crisis: float | None = None
    crisis_vol_multipliers: float | np.ndarray | None = None
    shock_dtype: type[np.float32] | type[np.float64] = np.float32
    _cholesky_transpose: np.ndarray = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _volatilities64: np.ndarray = field(init=False, repr=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        factor_ids = np.asarray(self.factor_ids, dtype=np.int32)
        if factor_ids.ndim != 1:
            raise ValueError("factor_ids must be 1D")

        cholesky_matrix = np.asarray(self.cholesky_matrix, dtype=np.float64)
        if cholesky_matrix.ndim != 2:
            raise ValueError("cholesky_matrix must be 2D")
        if cholesky_matrix.shape[0] != cholesky_matrix.shape[1]:
            raise ValueError("cholesky_matrix must be square")
        if factor_ids.shape[0] != cholesky_matrix.shape[0]:
            raise ValueError(
                "factor_ids length must match cholesky_matrix dimension"
            )
        if not np.isfinite(cholesky_matrix).all():
            raise ValueError("cholesky_matrix must contain only finite values")

        volatilities = np.asarray(self.volatilities, dtype=np.float64)
        if volatilities.ndim != 1:
            raise ValueError("volatilities must be 1D")
        if volatilities.shape[0] != cholesky_matrix.shape[0]:
            raise ValueError(
                "volatilities length must match cholesky_matrix dimension"
            )
        if not np.isfinite(volatilities).all():
            raise ValueError("volatilities must contain only finite values")
        if np.any(volatilities <= 0.0):
            raise ValueError("volatilities must be strictly positive")

        total_scenarios = int(self.total_scenarios)
        if total_scenarios <= 0:
            raise ValueError("total_scenarios must be positive")

        if self.seed is not None and not isinstance(
            self.seed, (int, np.integer)
        ):
            raise TypeError("seed must be an integer or None")

        if self.shock_dtype not in (np.float32, np.float64):
            raise TypeError("shock_dtype must be np.float32 or np.float64")

        if self.distribution_kind == "student_t":
            if self.student_t_df is None:
                raise ValueError("student_t_df is required for student_t")
            if not np.isfinite(self.student_t_df) or self.student_t_df <= 2.0:
                raise ValueError(
                    "student_t_df must be finite and greater than 2"
                )

        if self.distribution_kind == "variance_gamma":
            if self.vg_nu is None:
                raise ValueError("vg_nu is required for variance_gamma")
            if not np.isfinite(self.vg_nu) or self.vg_nu <= 0.0:
                raise ValueError("vg_nu must be finite and positive")

        if self.distribution_kind == "regime_switching_gmm":
            if self.prob_crisis is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "prob_crisis is required for regime_switching_gmm"
                )
            if not np.isfinite(self.prob_crisis):
                raise ValueError("prob_crisis must be finite")
            if not 0.0 <= self.prob_crisis <= 1.0:
                raise ValueError("prob_crisis must be between 0 and 1")
            if self.crisis_vol_multipliers is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "crisis_vol_multipliers is required for regime_switching_gmm"
                )

            if isinstance(
                self.crisis_vol_multipliers,
                (int, float, np.integer, np.floating),
            ):
                crisis_mult = float(self.crisis_vol_multipliers)
                if not np.isfinite(crisis_mult) or crisis_mult <= 0.0:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "crisis_vol_multipliers must be finite and positive"
                    )
                object.__setattr__(self, "crisis_vol_multipliers", crisis_mult)
            else:
                crisis_mult = np.asarray(
                    self.crisis_vol_multipliers, dtype=np.float64
                )
                if crisis_mult.ndim != 1:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "crisis_vol_multipliers must be scalar or 1D"
                    )
                if crisis_mult.shape[0] != cholesky_matrix.shape[0]:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "crisis_vol_multipliers length must match cholesky_matrix dimension"
                    )
                if not np.isfinite(crisis_mult).all() or np.any(
                    crisis_mult <= 0.0
                ):
                    raise ValueError(
                        "crisis_vol_multipliers must contain only positive finite values"
                    )
                object.__setattr__(self, "crisis_vol_multipliers", crisis_mult)

        object.__setattr__(self, "factor_ids", factor_ids)
        object.__setattr__(
            self, "cholesky_matrix", np.ascontiguousarray(cholesky_matrix)
        )
        object.__setattr__(self, "volatilities", volatilities)
        object.__setattr__(self, "total_scenarios", total_scenarios)
        object.__setattr__(
            self, "_cholesky_transpose", np.ascontiguousarray(cholesky_matrix.T)
        )
        object.__setattr__(self, "_volatilities64", volatilities)

    @classmethod
    def standard_normal(
        cls,
        factor_ids: np.ndarray,
        cholesky_matrix: np.ndarray,
        volatilities: np.ndarray,
        total_scenarios: int,
        seed: int | None = None,
        shock_dtype: type[np.float32] | type[np.float64] = np.float32,
    ) -> ParametricMCScenarioGenerator:
        return cls(
            factor_ids=factor_ids,
            cholesky_matrix=cholesky_matrix,
            volatilities=volatilities,
            total_scenarios=total_scenarios,
            distribution_kind="standard_normal",
            seed=seed,
            shock_dtype=shock_dtype,
        )

    @classmethod
    def student_t(
        cls,
        df: float,
        factor_ids: np.ndarray,
        cholesky_matrix: np.ndarray,
        volatilities: np.ndarray,
        total_scenarios: int,
        seed: int | None = None,
        shock_dtype: type[np.float32] | type[np.float64] = np.float32,
    ) -> ParametricMCScenarioGenerator:
        return cls(
            factor_ids=factor_ids,
            cholesky_matrix=cholesky_matrix,
            volatilities=volatilities,
            total_scenarios=total_scenarios,
            distribution_kind="student_t",
            seed=seed,
            student_t_df=float(df),
            shock_dtype=shock_dtype,
        )

    @classmethod
    def laplace(
        cls,
        factor_ids: np.ndarray,
        cholesky_matrix: np.ndarray,
        volatilities: np.ndarray,
        total_scenarios: int,
        seed: int | None = None,
        shock_dtype: type[np.float32] | type[np.float64] = np.float32,
    ) -> ParametricMCScenarioGenerator:
        return cls(
            factor_ids=factor_ids,
            cholesky_matrix=cholesky_matrix,
            volatilities=volatilities,
            total_scenarios=total_scenarios,
            distribution_kind="laplace",
            seed=seed,
            shock_dtype=shock_dtype,
        )

    @classmethod
    def variance_gamma(
        cls,
        vg_nu: float,
        factor_ids: np.ndarray,
        cholesky_matrix: np.ndarray,
        volatilities: np.ndarray,
        total_scenarios: int,
        seed: int | None = None,
        shock_dtype: type[np.float32] | type[np.float64] = np.float32,
    ) -> ParametricMCScenarioGenerator:
        return cls(
            factor_ids=factor_ids,
            cholesky_matrix=cholesky_matrix,
            volatilities=volatilities,
            total_scenarios=total_scenarios,
            distribution_kind="variance_gamma",
            seed=seed,
            vg_nu=float(vg_nu),
            shock_dtype=shock_dtype,
        )

    @classmethod
    def regime_switching_gmm(
        cls,
        prob_crisis: float,
        crisis_vol_multipliers: float | np.ndarray,
        factor_ids: np.ndarray,
        cholesky_matrix: np.ndarray,
        volatilities: np.ndarray,
        total_scenarios: int,
        seed: int | None = None,
        shock_dtype: type[np.float32] | type[np.float64] = np.float32,
    ) -> ParametricMCScenarioGenerator:
        return cls(
            factor_ids=factor_ids,
            cholesky_matrix=cholesky_matrix,
            volatilities=volatilities,
            total_scenarios=total_scenarios,
            distribution_kind="regime_switching_gmm",
            seed=seed,
            prob_crisis=float(prob_crisis),
            crisis_vol_multipliers=crisis_vol_multipliers,
            shock_dtype=shock_dtype,
        )

    def _apply_distribution_scaling(
        self,
        *,
        rng: np.random.Generator,
        shocks: np.ndarray,
        gmm_sliced_multipliers: float | np.ndarray | None,
    ) -> None:
        if self.distribution_kind == "standard_normal":
            return

        elif self.distribution_kind == "student_t":
            assert self.student_t_df is not None
            chi_square = rng.chisquare(
                df=self.student_t_df, size=shocks.shape[0]
            )
            row_scale = np.sqrt((self.student_t_df - 2.0) / chi_square)
            np.multiply(shocks, row_scale[:, None], out=shocks)

        elif self.distribution_kind == "laplace":
            exponential_mix = rng.exponential(scale=1.0, size=shocks.shape[0])
            row_scale = np.sqrt(exponential_mix)
            np.multiply(shocks, row_scale[:, None], out=shocks)

        elif self.distribution_kind == "variance_gamma":
            assert self.vg_nu is not None
            gamma_mix = rng.gamma(
                shape=1.0 / self.vg_nu, scale=self.vg_nu, size=shocks.shape[0]
            )
            row_scale = np.sqrt(gamma_mix)
            np.multiply(shocks, row_scale[:, None], out=shocks)

        elif self.distribution_kind == "regime_switching_gmm":
            assert self.prob_crisis is not None
            assert gmm_sliced_multipliers is not None
            crisis_mask = rng.random(shocks.shape[0]) < self.prob_crisis
            if np.any(crisis_mask):
                shocks[crisis_mask, :] *= gmm_sliced_multipliers

    def iter_batches(
        self,
        *,
        required_shift_type_id: np.ndarray,
        source_factor_ids: np.ndarray,
        shock_scale: np.ndarray,
        batch_size: int,
    ) -> Iterator[ScenarioSetBatch]:

        if batch_size <= 0:
            raise ValueError("batch_size must be positive")

        shock_source_ids, _, req_to_uix = build_shock_key_plan(
            required_shift_type_id=required_shift_type_id,
            source_factor_ids=source_factor_ids,
        )

        K_total = int(self.cholesky_matrix.shape[0])
        K_req = int(shock_source_ids.shape[0])

        id_to_col = {int(fid): i for i, fid in enumerate(self.factor_ids)}
        col_indices = np.empty(K_req, dtype=np.int32)

        for i in range(K_req):
            fid = int(shock_source_ids[i])
            idx = id_to_col.get(fid)
            if idx is None:
                raise KeyError(
                    f"Required factor_id {fid} not found in parametric universe."
                )
            col_indices[i] = idx

        # --- Pre-slice the mathematical parameters ---

        # Slice cholesky
        L_T_sliced = np.ascontiguousarray(
            self._cholesky_transpose[:, col_indices]
        )

        # Slice vols
        vols_sliced = np.ascontiguousarray(self._volatilities64[col_indices])

        # Slice GMM multipliers
        gmm_mults_sliced = None
        if self.distribution_kind == "regime_switching_gmm":
            assert self.crisis_vol_multipliers is not None

            # Type-checker safe: Explicitly isolate the ndarray for slicing
            if isinstance(self.crisis_vol_multipliers, np.ndarray):
                gmm_mults_sliced = np.ascontiguousarray(
                    self.crisis_vol_multipliers[col_indices]
                )
            else:
                # Both floats and ints fall safely here
                gmm_mults_sliced = float(self.crisis_vol_multipliers)

        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed)

        # Raw normals MUST remain K_total wide to ensure correct cross-asset correlation
        work = np.empty((batch_size, K_total), dtype=np.float64, order="C")

        # Correlated output is now directly allocated at K_req width
        correlated = np.empty((batch_size, K_req), dtype=np.float64, order="C")

        scenario_offset = 0

        while scenario_offset < self.total_scenarios:
            scenario_count = min(
                batch_size, self.total_scenarios - scenario_offset
            )

            work_view = work[:scenario_count, :]
            correlated_view = correlated[:scenario_count, :]

            # 1. Draw raw (B, K_total)
            rng.standard_normal(size=work_view.shape, out=work_view)

            # 2. Optimized Matmul: (B, K_total) @ (K_total, K_req) -> (B, K_req)
            np.matmul(work_view, L_T_sliced, out=correlated_view)

            # 3. Apply distribution scaling and base volatilities to the smaller matrix
            self._apply_distribution_scaling(
                rng=rng,
                shocks=correlated_view,
                gmm_sliced_multipliers=gmm_mults_sliced,
            )
            np.multiply(correlated_view, vols_sliced, out=correlated_view)

            # 4. Final copy to Fortran contiguous target
            shocks = np.empty(
                (scenario_count, K_req), dtype=self.shock_dtype, order="F"
            )
            np.copyto(shocks, correlated_view, casting="same_kind")

            yield ScenarioSetBatch(
                shocks=shocks,
                required_to_shock_uix=req_to_uix,
                shock_scale=shock_scale,
                required_shift_type_id=required_shift_type_id,
                scenario_offset=scenario_offset,
                scenario_count_total=self.total_scenarios,
            )

            scenario_offset += scenario_count

```

### FILE: simlab\shock_plan\plan.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/simlab/shocks/plan.py

from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ShockComputePlan:
    cols_sorted: np.ndarray
    shift_types_sorted: np.ndarray
    inverse_permutation: np.ndarray


def build_shock_compute_plan(
    *,
    shock_source_ids: np.ndarray,
    shock_shift_type_ids: np.ndarray,
    id_to_col: Dict[int, int],
) -> ShockComputePlan:

    cols = np.array(
        [id_to_col[int(sid)] for sid in shock_source_ids],
        dtype=np.int32,
    )

    shift_types = shock_shift_type_ids.astype(np.int8, copy=False)

    order = np.argsort(cols)

    cols_sorted = cols[order]
    shift_sorted = shift_types[order]

    inv = np.empty_like(order)
    inv[order] = np.arange(order.shape[0])

    return ShockComputePlan(
        cols_sorted=cols_sorted,
        shift_types_sorted=shift_sorted,
        inverse_permutation=inv,
    )

```

```python
# src/riskquant/valuation/engine.py
from datetime import date

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, Callable, Type

# INTERNAL IMPORTS
from riskquant.portfolio.compiled import CompiledPortfolio
from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix

# BLOCKS
from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.spot_block import SpotBlock
from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.forward_block import (
    ForwardCurveBlock,
    ForwardNodeBlock,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.vanilla_european_option_block import (
    VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock,
    VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock,
)

# PRICERS
from riskquant.valuation.pricers.spot import price_spot_block
from riskquant.valuation.pricers.forward_node import price_forward_node_block
from riskquant.valuation.pricers.forward_curve import price_forward_curve_block
from riskquant.valuation.pricers.vanilla_european_option_node import (
    price_vanilla_european_option_node_block,
)
from riskquant.valuation.pricers.vanilla_european_option_curve import (
    price_vanilla_european_option_curve_block,
)


# PRICER DISPATCH MAP
BLOCK_PRICER_MAP: Dict[Type, Callable] = {
    SpotBlock: price_spot_block,
    ForwardNodeBlock: price_forward_node_block,
    ForwardCurveBlock: price_forward_curve_block,
    VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock: price_vanilla_european_option_node_block,
    VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock: price_vanilla_european_option_curve_block,
}


def run_valuation(
    *,
    portfolio: CompiledPortfolio,
    market_data_mtx: MarketDataMatrix,
) -> np.ndarray:
    n_trades = portfolio.total_trades
    n_scenarios = market_data_mtx.num_scenarios

    # np.empty avoids the O(N) zero-fill overhead of np.zeros
    total_pvs = np.empty((n_trades, n_scenarios), dtype=np.float64)

    # Local binding for maximum speed inside the loop
    # Make local copy of the pricer map to allow mutation (caching) without affecting global state outside this function
    pricer_map = dict(BLOCK_PRICER_MAP)

    cursor = 0  # Cursor to track the position in the total_pvs array

    for block in portfolio.iter_blocks():
        block_type = type(block)
        pricer = pricer_map.get(block_type)

        # Lazy-Caching Dispatch:
        # If not an exact match, find the subclass match and cache it for O(1) next time.
        if pricer is None:
            for cls, candidate in pricer_map.items():
                if isinstance(block, cls):
                    pricer = candidate
                    pricer_map[block_type] = (
                        pricer  # Mutate global map to cache the subclass
                    )
                    break
            else:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"No pricer mapped for block type: {block_type.__name__}"
                )

        # Execute the vectorized pricer
        pvs = pricer(block=block, market_data_mtx=market_data_mtx)

        # Slice-write PVs back contiguously using the cursor
        num_block_trades = block.num_trades
        total_pvs[cursor : cursor + num_block_trades, :] = pvs

        cursor += num_block_trades

    return total_pvs

```

### FILE: valuation\models\black_scholes.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/valuation/models/black_scholes.py
import numpy as np
from scipy.stats import norm


def fv_black_scholes_vanilla_3d(
    *,
    fwd: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, S)
    strike: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, 1)
    vol: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, S)
    tte: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, 1)
    is_call: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, 1)
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Future Value (Undiscounted) of N options across S scenarios.
    Uses the Black (1976) forward formula. Represents the expected payoff at settlement.
    """
    safe_tte = np.maximum(tte, 1e-9)
    std_dev = vol * np.sqrt(safe_tte)

    d1 = (np.log(fwd / strike) + 0.5 * (vol**2) * safe_tte) / std_dev
    d2 = d1 - std_dev

    cp = np.where(is_call == 1, 1.0, -1.0)

    phi_d1 = norm.cdf(cp * d1)
    phi_d2 = norm.cdf(cp * d2)

    return cp * (fwd * phi_d1 - strike * phi_d2)


def pv_black_scholes_vanilla_3d(
    *,
    fwd: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, S)
    strike: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, 1)
    vol: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, S)
    tte: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, 1)
    is_call: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, 1)
    df_settle: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N, S)
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Present Value of N options across S scenarios.
    """
    fv = fv_black_scholes_vanilla_3d(
        fwd=fwd,
        strike=strike,
        vol=vol,
        tte=tte,
        is_call=is_call,
    )

    return fv * df_settle

```

### FILE: valuation\pricers\forward_curve.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/valuation/pricers/forward_curve.py
import numpy as np
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.forward_block import ForwardCurveBlock
    from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix


def price_forward_curve_block(
    *,
    block: "ForwardCurveBlock",
    market_data_mtx: "MarketDataMatrix",
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    God-Tier Vectorized Forward Curve Pricer.
    Calculates PV = Qty * (Fwd * FX_u2s * DF_under - Strike * FX_k2s * DF_strike)
    Uses in-place mutations and sentinel-skipping to minimize RAM and maximize CPU efficiency.
    """
    # ==========================================
    # 1. UNDERLYING LEG (Allocates Master Array)
    # ==========================================
    # Fancy indexing returns a copy. We own this memory and will use it
    # as our running PV accumulator to save RAM.
    # Shape: (N, S)
    pvs = market_data_mtx.price_curve_grid[
        block.price_curve_uix,
        block.delivery_date_uix,
        :,
    ]

    # --- Apply FX (Underlying to Settlement) ---
    mask_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_fx_u):
        valid_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_u]
        fx_u = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_u, :]

        # In-place mathematical inversion
        inv_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_invert[mask_fx_u].astype(
            bool, copy=False
        )
        if np.any(inv_u):
            fx_u[inv_u, :] = 1.0 / fx_u[inv_u, :]

        # In-place multiplication into the master accumulator
        pvs[mask_fx_u, :] *= fx_u

    # --- Apply Discounting (Underlying) ---
    mask_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_u):
        valid_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_u]
        valid_settle_u = block.settlement_date_uix[mask_df_u]
        df_u = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[
            valid_df_u, valid_settle_u, :
        ]

        pvs[mask_df_u, :] *= df_u

    # ==========================================
    # 2. STRIKE LEG (Allocates Secondary Array)
    # ==========================================
    # We allocate exactly one temporary array for the strike leg.
    # Shape: (N, S)
    leg_strike = np.empty_like(pvs, dtype=np.float64)

    # Broadcast the 1D strike array across the S scenarios
    leg_strike[:] = block.strike[:, None]

    # --- Apply FX (Strike to Settlement) ---
    mask_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_fx_k):
        valid_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_k]
        fx_k = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_k, :]

        inv_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_invert[mask_fx_k].astype(
            bool, copy=False
        )
        if np.any(inv_k):
            fx_k[inv_k, :] = 1.0 / fx_k[inv_k, :]

        leg_strike[mask_fx_k, :] *= fx_k

    # --- Apply Discounting (Strike) ---
    mask_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_k):
        valid_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_k]
        valid_settle_k = block.settlement_date_uix[mask_df_k]
        df_k = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[
            valid_df_k, valid_settle_k, :
        ]

        leg_strike[mask_df_k, :] *= df_k

    # ==========================================
    # 3. FINAL PV ASSEMBLY
    # ==========================================
    # PV = (leg_under - leg_strike) * qty
    # All operations done in-place to avoid final allocations
    pvs -= leg_strike
    pvs *= block.qty[:, None]

    return pvs

```

```python
# src/riskquant/valuation/pricers/forward_node.py
import numpy as np
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.forward_block import ForwardNodeBlock
    from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix


def price_forward_node_block(
    *,
    block: "ForwardNodeBlock",
    market_data_mtx: "MarketDataMatrix",
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    God-Tier Vectorized Forward Node Pricer.
    Calculates PV = Qty * (Fwd * FX_u2s * DF_under - Strike * FX_k2s * DF_strike)
    Uses in-place mutations and sentinel-skipping to minimize RAM and maximize CPU efficiency.
    """
    # ==========================================
    # 1. UNDERLYING LEG (Allocates Master Array)
    # ==========================================
    # Fancy indexing returns a copy, so we own this memory.
    # We will use this as our running PV accumulator to save RAM.
    # Shape: (N, S)
    pvs = market_data_mtx.forward_nodes[block.price_uix, :]

    # --- Apply FX (Underlying to Settlement) ---
    mask_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_fx_u):
        valid_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_u]
        fx_u = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_u, :]

        # In-place mathematical inversion

            bool, copy=False

        if np.any(inv_u):
            fx_u[inv_u, :] = 1.0 / fx_u[inv_u, :]

        # In-place multiplication into the master accumulator
        pvs[mask_fx_u, :] *= fx_u

    # --- Apply Discounting (Underlying) ---
    mask_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_u):
        valid_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_u]
        valid_settle_u = block.settlement_date_uix[mask_df_u]
        df_u = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[
            valid_df_u, valid_settle_u, :
        ]

        pvs[mask_df_u, :] *= df_u

    # ==========================================
    # 2. STRIKE LEG (Allocates Secondary Array)
    # ==========================================
    # We allocate exactly one temporary array for the strike leg.
    # Shape: (N, S)
    leg_strike = np.empty_like(pvs, dtype=np.float64)

    # Broadcast the 1D strike array across the S scenarios
    leg_strike[:] = block.strike[:, None]

    # --- Apply FX (Strike to Settlement) ---
    mask_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_fx_k):
        valid_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_k]
        fx_k = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_k, :]

        inv_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_invert[mask_fx_k].astype(
            bool, copy=False
        )
        if np.any(inv_k):
            fx_k[inv_k, :] = 1.0 / fx_k[inv_k, :]

        leg_strike[mask_fx_k, :] *= fx_k

    # --- Apply Discounting (Strike) ---
    mask_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_k):
        valid_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_k]
        valid_settle_k = block.settlement_date_uix[mask_df_k]
        df_k = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[
            valid_df_k, valid_settle_k, :
        ]

        leg_strike[mask_df_k, :] *= df_k

    # ==========================================
    # 3. FINAL PV ASSEMBLY
    # ==========================================
    # PV = (leg_under - leg_strike) * qty
    # All operations done in-place to avoid final allocations
    pvs -= leg_strike
    pvs *= block.qty[:, None]

    return pvs
```

### FILE: valuation\pricers\spot.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/valuation/pricers/spot.py
"""Vectorized pricer for spot positions (SpotBlock) in the CompiledPortfolio framework."""
import numpy as np
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.spot_block import SpotBlock
    from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix


def price_spot_block(
    *,
    block: "SpotBlock",
    market_data_mtx: "MarketDataMatrix",
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    God-Tier Vectorized Spot Pricer.
    Calculates PV = Qty * Price * FX in-place to minimize RAM usage and GC overhead.
    """
    # ==========================================
    # 1. BASE PV (Allocates the Master Output Array)
    # ==========================================
    # Fancy indexing allocates the (M, S) array. We own this memory,
    # so we will mutate it directly to save RAM.
    pvs = market_data_mtx.prices[block.price_uix, :]

    # Scale by Quantity in-place
    pvs *= block.qty[:, None]

    # ==========================================
    # 2. FX TRANSLATION (Sentinel-Safe & In-Place)
    # ==========================================
    # Identify only the trades that require an FX conversion (uix >= 0)
    # This prevents the NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED sentinel (-6) from wrapping around the array
    fx_mask = block.fx_uix >= 0

    if np.any(fx_mask):
        valid_fx_uix = block.fx_uix[fx_mask]

        # Fetch only the FX rates we actually need
        fx_rates = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_uix, :]

        # Identify which of these specific valid FX rates need mathematical inversion
        inv_mask = block.fx_invert[fx_mask].astype(bool, copy=False)

        if np.any(inv_mask):
            # In-place mathematical inversion
            fx_rates[inv_mask, :] = 1.0 / fx_rates[inv_mask, :]

        # Multiply into the master PV array in-place
        pvs[fx_mask, :] *= fx_rates

    # ==========================================
    # 3. RETURN
    # ==========================================
    return pvs

```

### FILE: valuation\pricers\vanilla_european_option_curve.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/valuation/pricers/vanilla_european_option_curve.py
import numpy as np
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from riskquant.constants import MOCK_VOLATILITY

# FIXED: We use the Future Value (FV) kernel to maintain ownership of the memory
# and avoid passing massive df_settle arrays.
from riskquant.valuation.models.black_scholes import fv_black_scholes_vanilla_3d
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_defs import (
    interp_strike_linear_flat_extrap,
)
from riskquant.market_data.surfaces.y_axes_types import (
    SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_STRIKE,
    SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_LOG_STRIKE,
    SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_MONEYNESS_SPOT,
    SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_MONEYNESS_FWD,
    SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_LOG_MONEYNESS_SPOT,
    SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_LOG_MONEYNESS_FWD,
)

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.vanilla_european_option_block import (
        VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock,
    )
    from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix


def price_vanilla_european_option_curve_block(
    *,
    block: "VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock",
    market_data_mtx: "MarketDataMatrix",
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    God-Tier Vectorized Vanilla European Option Curve Pricer.
    Optimized for minimal allocations, in-place transformations, and zero redundant arrays.
    """

    # ==========================================
    # 1. FETCH FORWARD PRICES (The Master Accumulator)
    # ==========================================
    # Fancy indexing returns a copy. We own this memory.
    # Shape: (N, S)
    fwd_in_strike_ccy = market_data_mtx.price_curve_grid[
        block.underlying_price_curve_uix,
        block.delivery_date_uix,
        :,
    ]

    # Time to Expiry is standard across the block
    tte = market_data_mtx.tte_grid[block.expiry_date_uix][:, None]

    # ==========================================
    # 2. UNDERLYING FX & DISCOUNTING (In-Place)
    # ==========================================
    # Covered Interest Parity: Fwd = Fwd * Spot_FX * DF_under

    mask_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_fx_u):
        valid_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_u]
        fx_u = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_u, :]

        inv_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_invert[mask_fx_u].astype(
            bool, copy=False
        )
        if np.any(inv_u):
            fx_u[inv_u, :] = 1.0 / fx_u[inv_u, :]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_fx_u, :] *= fx_u

    mask_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_u):
        valid_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_u]
        valid_exp_u = block.expiry_date_uix[mask_df_u]
        df_u = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[valid_df_u, valid_exp_u, :]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_df_u, :] *= df_u

    # ==========================================
    # 3. STRIKE FX & DISCOUNTING (In-Place)
    # ==========================================
    # Covered Interest Parity: Fwd = Fwd / (Spot_FX * DF_strike)

    mask_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix >= 0
    fx_k_cache = None
    if np.any(mask_fx_k):
        valid_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_k]
        fx_k_cache = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_k, :]

        inv_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_invert[mask_fx_k].astype(
            bool, copy=False
        )
        if np.any(inv_k):
            fx_k_cache[inv_k, :] = 1.0 / fx_k_cache[inv_k, :]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_fx_k, :] /= fx_k_cache

    mask_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_k):
        valid_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_k]
        valid_exp_k = block.expiry_date_uix[mask_df_k]
        df_k_exp = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[
            valid_df_k, valid_exp_k, :
        ]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_df_k, :] /= df_k_exp

    # ==========================================
    # 4. Y-COORDINATE TRANSFORMER
    # ==========================================
    K = block.strike[:, None]
    F = fwd_in_strike_ccy

    # Pre-allocate target array. Unused slots will be overwritten.
    y_target = np.empty_like(F, dtype=np.float64)
    y_types = block.vol_surface_y_axis_type_id

    # --- STRIKE ---
    m_strike = y_types == SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_STRIKE
    if np.any(m_strike):
        y_target[m_strike] = K[m_strike]

    # --- LOG STRIKE ---
    m_log_strike = y_types == SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_LOG_STRIKE
    if np.any(m_log_strike):
        y_target[m_log_strike] = np.log(K[m_log_strike])

    # --- MONEYNESS (Fwd & Spot Combined) ---
    m_fwd_mon = (y_types == SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_MONEYNESS_FWD) | (
        y_types == SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_MONEYNESS_SPOT
    )
    if np.any(m_fwd_mon):
        y_target[m_fwd_mon] = K[m_fwd_mon] / F[m_fwd_mon]

    # --- LOG MONEYNESS (Fwd & Spot Combined) ---
    m_log_fwd_mon = (y_types == SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_LOG_MONEYNESS_FWD) | (
        y_types == SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_LOG_MONEYNESS_SPOT
    )
    if np.any(m_log_fwd_mon):
        y_target[m_log_fwd_mon] = np.log(K[m_log_fwd_mon] / F[m_log_fwd_mon])

    # ==========================================
    # 5. DYNAMIC STRIKE INTERPOLATION (PHASE 2)
    # ==========================================
    if market_data_mtx.vol_surfaces.size == 0:
        vols = np.full_like(F, MOCK_VOLATILITY, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        vols = interp_strike_linear_flat_extrap(
            pillars=market_data_mtx.vol_surface_strikes[block.vol_surface_uix],
            values=market_data_mtx.vol_surfaces[
                block.vol_surface_uix, block.expiry_date_uix
            ],
            targets=y_target,
        )

    # ==========================================
    # 6. EXECUTE BLACK-SCHOLES (FV)
    # ==========================================
    # This allocates our final PV array as Future Value.
    pvs = fv_black_scholes_vanilla_3d(
        fwd=fwd_in_strike_ccy,
        strike=K,
        vol=vols,
        tte=tte,
        is_call=block.is_call[:, None],
    )

    # ==========================================
    # 7. FINAL PV ASSEMBLY (In-Place)
    # ==========================================
    # Discount the Future Value back to today
    if np.any(mask_df_k):
        valid_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_k]
        valid_stl_k = block.settlement_date_uix[mask_df_k]
        df_settle = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[
            valid_df_k, valid_stl_k, :
        ]

        pvs[mask_df_k, :] *= df_settle

    # Translate PV from Strike Currency to Settlement Currency
    if fx_k_cache is not None:
        pvs[mask_fx_k, :] *= fx_k_cache

    # Scale by quantity
    pvs *= block.qty[:, None]

    return pvs

```

### FILE: valuation\pricers\vanilla_european_option_node.py ###
```python
# src/riskquant/valuation/pricers/vanilla_european_option_node.py
import numpy as np
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

# Note: We import the Future Value (FV) kernel to maintain ownership of the memory
# so we can apply final discounting and FX conversions in-place.
from riskquant.valuation.models.black_scholes import fv_black_scholes_vanilla_3d

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.vanilla_european_option_block import (
        VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock,
    )
    from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix


def price_vanilla_european_option_node_block(
    *,
    block: "VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock",
    market_data_mtx: "MarketDataMatrix",
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    God-Tier Vectorized Vanilla European Option Node Pricer.
    Uses in-place mutations, sentinel-skipping, and caching to minimize RAM.
    """
    # ==========================================
    # 1. FETCH UNDERLYING (Allocates Master Array)
    # ==========================================
    # Fancy indexing returns a copy. We own this memory and will use it
    # to synthesize the Forward Price in the Strike Currency in-place.
    fwd_in_strike_ccy = market_data_mtx.forward_nodes[
        block.underlying_price_uix, :
    ]

    # ==========================================
    # 2. SYNTHESIZE FORWARD FX (In-Place)
    # ==========================================

    # --- Underlying FX ---
    mask_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_fx_u):
        valid_fx_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_u]
        fx_u = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_u, :]

        inv_u = block.fx_under_to_settle_invert[mask_fx_u].astype(
            bool, copy=False
        )
        if np.any(inv_u):
            fx_u[inv_u, :] = 1.0 / fx_u[inv_u, :]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_fx_u, :] *= fx_u

    # --- Underlying Discount (Expiry) ---
    mask_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_u):
        valid_df_u = block.under_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_u]
        valid_exp_u = block.expiry_date_uix[mask_df_u]
        df_u = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[valid_df_u, valid_exp_u, :]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_df_u, :] *= df_u

    # --- Strike FX (Cached for later use) ---
    mask_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix >= 0
    fx_k_cache = None
    if np.any(mask_fx_k):
        valid_fx_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_uix[mask_fx_k]
        fx_k_cache = market_data_mtx.fx_spots[valid_fx_k, :]

        inv_k = block.fx_strike_to_settle_invert[mask_fx_k].astype(
            bool, copy=False
        )
        if np.any(inv_k):
            fx_k_cache[inv_k, :] = 1.0 / fx_k_cache[inv_k, :]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_fx_k, :] /= fx_k_cache

    # --- Strike Discount (Expiry) ---
    mask_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix >= 0
    if np.any(mask_df_k):
        valid_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_k]
        valid_exp_k = block.expiry_date_uix[mask_df_k]
        df_k = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[valid_df_k, valid_exp_k, :]

        fwd_in_strike_ccy[mask_df_k, :] /= df_k

    # ==========================================
    # 3. EXECUTE BLACK-SCHOLES (FV)
    # ==========================================
    # This allocates our final PV array. We fetch TTE and Vols natively here.
    pvs = fv_black_scholes_vanilla_3d(
        fwd=fwd_in_strike_ccy,
        strike=block.strike[:, None],
        vol=market_data_mtx.vol_nodes[block.vol_uix, :],
        tte=market_data_mtx.tte_grid[block.expiry_date_uix][:, None],
        is_call=block.is_call[:, None],
    )

    # ==========================================
    # 4. FINAL PV ASSEMBLY (In-Place)
    # ==========================================
    # Discount the Future Value back to today
    if np.any(mask_df_k):
        # Note: We reuse the mask_df_k since the settlement discount curve
        # is the same curve used for the strike leg
        valid_df_k = block.strike_discount_curve_uix[mask_df_k]
        valid_stl_k = block.settlement_date_uix[mask_df_k]
        df_settle = market_data_mtx.discount_factor_grid[
            valid_df_k, valid_stl_k, :
        ]

        pvs[mask_df_k, :] *= df_settle

    # Translate PV from Strike Currency to Settlement Currency
    if fx_k_cache is not None:
        pvs[mask_fx_k, :] *= fx_k_cache

    # Scale by quantity
    pvs *= block.qty[:, None]

    return pvs

```
